[2026-04-30 11:04:04] 天使: 恢复算法的 10 分钟 benchmark，它的枚举是一个什么样的水平呢？75,000 多组是一个什么样的水平？由于我们处理的是一个矩阵，然后使用汉明距离作为偏差，也就是说，我们这一个基准允许的最大偏差大概是一个怎样的水平？
[2026-04-30 11:05:06] Codex: 天使，75,000 多组的含义是：**AI 已经把恢复故事压缩成 12 列候选矩阵之后，本地在 10 分钟内能枚举约 7.5 万个“12 词组合”。**\n\n它不是从 `2048^12` 里乱撞，而是在一个小矩阵里按“离第一候选有多远”从近到远搜。\n\n**偏差定义**\n假设 12 个词，每列 AI 给 `K` 个候选：\n\n```text\n第1列: [豆腐脑, 豆花, 豆浆]\n第2列: [雨伞, 雨衣, 雨鞋]\n...\n第12列: [...]\n```\n\n每列第一个词是 rank-0，也就是 AI 最相信的答案。  \n汉明距离 `D=4` 的意思是：**最多允许 4 列不用第一候选，改用第二/第三/...候选。**\n\n所以它衡量的是：\n\n- 不是字面错别字距离\n- 不是中文语义距离\n- 是“12 个词位里，有几个词位的第一候选猜错了”\n\n**75,000 组大概能覆盖什么水平**\n\n按这次 benchmark：\n\n| 每列候选数 K | 10 分钟能完整覆盖的偏差 | 含义 |\n|---:|---:|---|\n| K=3 | D=5 | 12 个词里最多 5 个词的第一候选错了，但正确词在该列前 3 名内 |\n| K=4 | D=4 | 12 个词里最多 4 个词的第一候选错了，但正确词在该列前 4 名内 |\n| K=5 | D=3 | 12 个词里最多 3 个词的第一候选错了，但正确词在该列前 5 名内 |\n\n更直观一点：\n\n- `K=3, D=5`：已经是 **接近半数词位的一候选可错**，容错很强\n- `K=4, D=4`：允许 **三分之一词位的一候选可错**\n- `K=5, D=3`：允许 **四分之一词位的一候选可错**\n\n**为什么 D 再高会爆炸**\n\n组合数公式是：\n\n```text\nΣ C(12,d) * (K-1)^d, d=0..D\n```\n\n例如：\n\n- `K=3, D=5`：35,313 组，10 分钟内很稳\n- `K=3, D=6`：94,449 组，略超过 10 分钟，约 12.5 分钟\n- `K=4, D=4`：46,666 组，10 分钟内稳\n- `K=4, D=5`：239,122 组，约 32 分钟\n- `K=5, D=4`：141,905 组，约 19 分钟\n\n所以核心结论是：\n\n**当前算法在 10 分钟预算内，比较适合“每列 3-4 个高质量候选，允许 4-5 个词位的一候选错误”。**\n\n如果恢复故事太模糊，导致每列要给 5 个以上候选，或者超过 4-5 个词位都不确定，就应该让用户补充描述，而不是硬跑全空间。  \n这个设计方向是合理的：本地枚举负责最后一公里，AI/交互负责把矩阵压窄。
[2026-04-30 11:08:29] 天使: 如果说要用上 GPU，我认为这类计算如果能用上 GPU 的话，它的速度肯定会提升很多。\n\n由于我们的恢复端计算量是在客户的电脑上，也是运行在 Flutter 的程序里面，能否外挂一个其他语言、更高效率的编译插件？当然 Flutter 内部能处理也可以。\n\n如果能用到 GPU 的话，基准会不会再次提高？甚至能把矩阵（整个矩阵的汉明距离）全部算尽？
[2026-04-30 11:08:52] Codex: 天使，我先看一下当前恢复算法具体把时间花在哪一步，再回答 GPU/外挂插件是否值得做。这个问题的关键不是“能不能并行”，而是当前瓶颈是不是适合 GPU。

/// 在汉明距离 d 层搜索，把所有组合分给 nWorkers 个 worker isolate。\n  ///\n  /// 每个 worker 收到自己的分片范围（combo_index 区间），独立枚举 + 检查\n  /// 校验位 + PBKDF2 + secp256k1，找到匹配回传种子。任一 worker 命中后\n  /// 取消其他 worker。\n receivePort = ReceivePort();\n    final completer = Completer<MnemonicSeed?>();\n    final isolates = <Isolate>[];\n    var done = 0;\n\n    void finish(MnemonicSeed? result) {\n      if (completer.isCompleted) return;\n      completer.complete(result);\n      // 关停所有 worker\n      for (final iso in isolates) {\n        iso.kill(priority: Isolate.immediate);\n      }\n      receivePort.close();\n    }\n\n    receivePort.listen((msg) {\n      if (msg is Map) {\n        if (msg['type'] == 'progress') {\n          attemptedCounter.add(msg['count'] as int);\n        } else (msg['type'] == 'done') {\n          done++;\n          if (done >= nWorkers) {\n            // 所有 worker 报告完成而没有命中\n            if (!completer.isCompleted) finish(null);\n          }\n        }\n _WorkerArgs(\n          sendPort: receivePort.sendPort,\n          flat: flat,\n          d: d,\n          k: kPerColumn,\n          shardIndex: w,\n          shardCount: nWorkers,\n 离线场景：用一个全局缓存暴露\"已知的目标公钥哈希集合\"。\n  /// 临时实现：返回空集，让 worker 把所有\"校验位 PASS\"的候选都回传。\n  ///\n  /// 长期方案：增加一个 [Recovery.knownPublicKeyHashes] 字段，构造时由\n  /// 调用方提供（比如 IdentityRepository.loginWithStory 的 checker 改成\n  /// 直接传一组哈希）。\n  Future<Set<String>> _resolveKnownHashes() async {\n    // 由调用方注入。当前实现：空集，全部校验位 PASS 的候选都回传给主 isolate。\n    return _RecoveryConfig.instance.knownTargetHashes;\n  }\n\n  static int _defaultWorkerCount() {\n    final cores = Platform.numberOfProcessors;\n    if (cores <= 2) return 1;\n    return cores - 1;\n  }\n}\n\n/// 进度报告。\nclass RecoveryProgress {\n  RecoveryProgress({\n    required this.currentHammingDistance,\n    required this.attempted,\n    required this.elapsedMs,\n  });\n  final int currentHammingDistance;\n  final int attempted;\n  final int elapsedMs;\n}\n\n/// 共享配置（调用方在 tryRecover 之前用 [setKnownHashes] 注入目标哈希）。\nclass _RecoveryConfig {\n  _RecoveryConfig._();\n  static final instance = _RecoveryConfig._();\n  Set<String> knownTargetHashes = const {};\n}\n\n/// 调用方在尝试恢复前必须调一次本函数把\"目标公钥哈希集合\"注入。\n/// worker isolate 派生候选后用这个集合做哈希比对，命中才回传种子给主 isolate。\n///\n/// 离线场景：本地存的旧公钥的 SHA-256 哈希\n/// 在线场景：先调 pubkey-service 把候选范围拉到本地，然后注入\nvoid setKnownPublicKeyHashes(Set<String> hashes) {\n  _RecoveryConfig.instance.knownTargetHashes = Set.unmodifiable(hashes);\n}\n\n// ===========================================================================\n//  Worker isolate\n// ===========================================================================\n\nclass _WorkerArgs {\n  _WorkerArgs({\n    required this.sendPort,\n    required this.flat,\n    required this.d,\n    required this.k,\n    required this.shardIndex,\n    required this.shardCount,\n    required this.knownHashes,\n    required this.deadlineEpochMs,\n  });\n\n  final SendPort sendPort;\n  final Int32List flat;\n  final int d;\n  final int k;\n  final int shardIndex;\n  final int shardCount;\n  final Set<String> knownHashes;\n  final int deadlineEpochMs;\n}\n\nvoid _workerEntry(_WorkerArgs args) {\n  final cIdxMax = _binom(kMnemonicLength, args.d);\n  // worker 处理 cIdx 中 cIdx % shardCount == shardIndex 的部分\n  var localCount = 0;\n  const reportEvery = 1000;\n\n  for (var cIdx = args.shardIndex; cIdx < cIdxMax; cIdx += args.shardCount) {\n    if (DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch >= args.deadlineEpochMs) break;\n\n    final cols = _comboAt(kMnemonicLength, args.d, cIdx);\n    final substMax = _pow(args.k - 1, args.d);\n\n    for (var sIdx = 0; sIdx < substMax; sIdx++) {\n      // 为每列计算 rank：默认 0；被选中的 d 列各自取 1..K-1\n      final ids = List<int>.generate(\n        kMnemonicLength,\n        (i) => args.flat[i * args.k],\n      );\n      var s = sIdx;\n      for (final c in cols) tryMnemonicFromIds(ids);\n      if (seed != null) {\n        final pair = HanakoKeyPair.fromPrivateKeyBytes(seed.privateKeyBytes);\n        // 如果调用方注入了已知哈希集合，过滤；否则放过让主 isolate checker 决定。\n        if (args.knownHashes.isEmpty || args.knownHashes.contains(pair.publicKeyHash)) {\n          args.sendPort.send({'type': 'hit', 'ids': ids});\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n\n      localCount++;\n      if (localCount % reportEvery == 0) {\n        args.sendPort.send({'type': 'progress', 'count': reportEvery});\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  if (localCount % reportEvery != 0) {\n    args.sendPort.send({'type': 'progress', 'count': localCount % reportEvery});\n  }\n  args.sendPort.send({'type': 'done'});\n}\n\n// ===========================================================================\n//  组合枚举：cIdx → cols\n// ===========================================================================\n\n/// 给定 [n] 取 [k]，返回字典序第 [idx] 个组合（idx 0-based）。\nList<int> _comboAt(int n, int k, int idx) {\n  if (k == 0) return const [];\n  final out = List<int>.filled(k, 0);\n  var c = idx;\n  var start = 0;\n  for (var i = 0; i < k; i++) {\n    for (var x = start; x < n; x++) {\n      final remaining = _binom(n - x - 1, k - i - 1);\n      if (c < remaining) {\n        out[i] = x;\n        start = x + 1;\n        break;\n      }\n      c -= remaining;\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nint _binom(int n, int k) {\n  if (k < 0 || k > n) return 0;\n  if (k == 0 || k == n) return 1;\n  var r = 1;\n  final kk = k > n - k ? n - k : k;\n  for (var i = 0; i < kk; i++) {\n    r = r * (n - i) ~/ (i + 1);\n  }\n  return r;\n}\n\nint _pow(int base, int exp) {\n  var r = 1;\n  for (var i = 0; i < exp; i++) {\n    r *= base;\n  }\n  return r;\n}\n\n// ===========================================================================\n//  原子计数器（跨 isolate 通过消息累加，主 isolate 持有真实状态）\n// });\n}\n\nint _searchSpace(int cols, int k, int dMax) {\n  // Σ_{d=0..dMax} C(cols,d) · (k-1)^d\n  var total = 0;\n  for (var d = 0; d <= dMax; d++) {\n    total += _binom(cols, d) * _pow((k - 1), 注册期：随机熵 → 12 中文名词（有序）→ 用户保存\n//   - 恢复期：12 中文名词（有序）→ 还原同一私钥\n//   - 字典与算法版本固定后，同一组词永远派生出同一私钥\n//\n// 安全说明：\n//   - mnemonic 字符串本身就是密钥的\"完整形态\"，泄漏即等同私钥泄漏；\n//   - 内存中持有 mnemonic 的时间应尽量短，用完即清；\n//   - 如需更高安全级别可加 passphrase（\"BIP-39 25th word\"），目前留空。\n\nimport 'dart:convert';\nimport 'dart:math';\nimport 'dart:typed_data';\n\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha256.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha512.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/key_derivators/pbkdf2.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/macs/hmac.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/pointycastle.dart' show Pbkdf2Parameters;\n\nimport 'word_dict.dart';\n\n/// 助记词长度（固定 12 词 = 132 bit = 128 entropy + 4 checksum）。\nconst int kMnemonicLength = 12;\n\n/// 熵字节数（128 bit）。\nconst int kEntropyBytes = 16;\n\n/// 一组助记词及其派生产物。\nclass MnemonicSeed {\n  MnemonicSeed({\n    required this.words,\n    required this.entropy,\n    required this.seed,\n  });\n\n  /// 12 个中文名词（有序）。\n  final List<String> words;\n\n  /// 原始熵（16 字节）。\n  final Uint8List entropy;\n\n  /// PBKDF2 派生的 64 字节种子。\n  final Uint8List seed;\n\n  /// 取 secp256k1 私钥（seed 前 32 字节）。\n  Uint8List get privateKeyBytes => Uint8List.fromList(seed.sublist(0, 32));\n\n  /// 用空格拼接的助记词原文（PBKDF2 的输入格式）。\n  String get mnemonicString => words.join(' ');\n}\n\n/// 注册期入口：生成全新助记词与对应种子。\n///\n/// 流程：\n///   1. 调系统安全 PRNG 拉 16 字节熵；\n///   2. 计算 SHA-256 校验和的高 4 bit 拼到末尾，得到 132 bit；\n///   3. 按 11 bit 切片得到 12 个 ID，查字典得 12 个中文名词；\n///   4. PBKDF2 派生 64 字节种子。\nMnemonicSeed generateMnemonic() {\n  if (hanakoWordlistSize < kMnemonicLength) {\n    throw StateError(\n      '字典词数过少（$hanakoWordlistSize），无法生成助记词。'\n      '请扩充 word_dict.dart 至少到 $kMnemonicLength 词以上。',\n    );\n  }\n  final entropy = _secureRandom(kEntropyBytes);\n  final words = _entropyToWords(entropy);\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n}\n\n/// 恢复期入口：把已知 12 词还原成同一种子。\n///\n/// 抛 [ArgumentError]：\n///   - 词数不对；\n///   - 词不在字典里；\n///   - 校验位不对（说明用户记错了某个词）。\nMnemonicSeed mnemonicFromWords(List<String> words) {\n  if (words.length != kMnemonicLength) {\n    throw ArgumentError('助记词必须为 $kMnemonicLength 个词，收到 ${words.length}');\n  }\n  final ids = <int>[];\n  for (final w in words) {\n    final id = idByWord(w);\n    if (id == null) {\n      throw ArgumentError('词不在字典里：$w');\n    }\n    ids.add(id);\n  }\n  final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n}\n\n/// 用一组候选 ID 列表尝试恢复（恢复期 LLM 解析后用），\n/// 不抛错，校验失败返回 null，便于穷举循环外层判断。\nMnemonicSeed? tryMnemonicFromIds(List<int> ids) {\n  if (ids.length != kMnemonicLength) return null;\n  for (final id in ids) {\n    if (id < 0 || id >= hanakoWordlistSize) return null;\n  }\n  // 校验逻辑藏在 _idsToEntropy 内部，校验失败抛 [FormatException]。\n  try {\n    final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\n    final words = ids.map((i) => wordById(i)!).toList();\n    final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n    return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n  } on FormatException {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// entropy(16B) → 12 个 ID → 12 个词。\nList<String> _entropyToWords(Uint8List entropy) {\n  // 1. 算 4-bit 校验位（SHA-256 首字节高 4 bit）。\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(entropy);\n  final checksum = hash[0] >> 4; // 0..15\n  // 2. 把 entropy + checksum 拼成 132-bit 大整数 ↔ BigInt 操作最直观。\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\n  for (final b in entropy) {\n    bits = (bits << 8) | BigInt.from(b);\n  }\n  bits = (bits << 4) | BigInt.from(checksum);\n  // 3. 11-bit 一组切，从高到低输出 12 个 ID。\n  final ids = <int>[];\n  for (var i = kMnemonicLength - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    final shift = 11 * i;\n    final mask = BigInt.from(0x7ff); // 11 bit\n    final id = ((bits >> shift) & mask).toInt();\n    ids.add(id);\n  }\n  return ids.map((id) {\n    final w = wordById(id);\n    if (w == null) {\n      throw StateError('字典越界：ID $id（字典大小 $hanakoWordlistSize）');\n    }\n    return w;\n  }).toList();\n}\n\n/// 12 个 ID → entropy(16B)，同时校验 4-bit 校验位。\nUint8List _idsToEntropy(List<int> ids) {\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\n  for (final id in ids) {\n    bits = (bits << 11) | BigInt.from(id);\n  }\n  // 拆出末尾 4 bit 校验位。\n  final checksumExpected = (bits & BigInt.from(0xf)).toInt();\n  bits = bits >> 4;\n  // 高 128 bit 还原成 16 字节。\n  final out = Uint8List(kEntropyBytes);\n  for (var i = kEntropyBytes - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    out[i] = (bits & BigInt.from(0xff)).toInt();\n    bits = bits >> 8;\n  }\n  // 重新计算校验位比对。\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(out);\n  final checksumActual = hash[0] >> 4;\n  if (checksumExpected != checksumActual) {\n    throw const FormatException('助记词校验位不匹配（很可能某个词记错了）');\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/// PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(mnemonic, \"mnemonic\"+passphrase, 2048, 64)\n/// — BIP-39 标准 seed 派生流程。passphrase 留空。\nUint8List _mnemonicToSeed(String mnemonic, {String passphrase = ''}) {\n  final pbkdf2 = PBKDF2KeyDerivator(HMac(SHA512Digest(), 128))\n    ..init(Pbkdf2Parameters(\n      utf8.encode('mnemonic$passphrase'),\n      2048,\n      64,\n    ));\n  return pbkdf2.process(utf8.encode(mnemonic));\n}\n\nUint8List _secureRandom(int n) {\n  final rng = Random.secure();\n  final out = Uint8List(n);\n  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    out[i] = rng.nextInt(256);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\r\n"}

SHA-256 公钥哈希\n//   5. set 比对\n//\n// 怎么用：\n//   flutter test test/recovery_benchmark.dart --plain-name benchmark\n//   或 release 模式：\n//   dart compile exe test/recovery_benchmark.dart -o build/bench.exe\n//\n// 给恢复算法的 K（每列候选数）和 D（汉明距离上限）设计提供基准。\n\nimport 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';\nimport 'package:hanako/identity/keypair.dart';\nimport micros/次。\ndouble _bench(int n, void Function() body) {\n  // warmup\n  for (var i = 0; i < n ~/ 10 + 1; i++) {\n    body();\n  }\n  final sw = Stopwatch()..start();\n  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n 输出）\n    final ids = mn.words.map((w) => idByWord(w)!).toList();\n\n    // ============ 各阶段单独计时 =============\n    print('\\n=== 单阶段基准 (取 100 次平均) ===');\n\n    // 阶段 1：BIP-39 校验位\n    final t1 = _bench(100, () {\n      tryMnemonicFromIds(ids);\n    });\n    print('  PBKDF2 + 校验位 (一次完整 mnemonic→seed): ${t1.toStringAsFixed(0)} µs');\n\n    // 阶段 2：仅 secp256k1 公钥派生（不含 PBKDF2）\n    final priv = mn.privateKeyBytes;\n    final t2 = _bench(100, () {\n      HanakoKeyPair.fromPrivateKeyBytes(priv);\n    });\n    print('  secp256k1 公钥派生:                       ${t2.toStringAsFixed(0)} µs');\n\n    // 阶段 3：SHA-256 公钥哈希\n    final pubBytes = pair.publicKeyBytes;\n    final t3 = _bench(1000, () {\n      SHA256Digest().process(pubBytes);\n    });\n    print('  SHA-256 公钥哈希:                         ${t3.toStringAsFixed(0)} µs');\n\n    // 阶段 4：set 比对\n    final t4 = _bench(100000, () {\n      targetSet.contains(targetHash);\n    });\n    print('  Set.contains:                             ${t4.toStringAsFixed(2)} µs');\n\n    // 全链路：模拟\"一次候选组合的完整 verify\"\n    print('\\n=== 全链路一次完整验证 (取 50 次平均) ===');\n    final tFull = _bench(50, () {\n      final seed = tryMnemonicFromIds(ids);\n        ${tFull.toStringAsFixed(0)} µs');\n    print('  → 每秒可跑:                                ${(1e6 / tFull).toStringAsFixed(0)} 次/s');\n\n    // ============ 推算搜索空间预算 =============\n    final perAttemptMs = tFull / 1000;\n    final budget5min = 5 * 60 * 1000 / perAttemptMs;\n    final budget10min = 10 * 60 * 1000 / perAttemptMs;\n\n    print('\\n=== 时间预算反推 ===');\n    print('  5 分钟可跑:  ${budget5min.toStringAsFixed(0)} 次完整尝试');\n    print('  10 分钟可跑: ${budget10min.toStringAsFixed(0)} 次完整尝试');\n\n    // 校验位过滤：只有 1/16 通过 BIP-39 校验，所以\"完整 PBKDF2\"的次数会减少。\n    // 但这里 tryMnemonicFromIds 本身已经做了校验位检查，校验失败时会立刻抛\n    // FormatException 不走 PBKDF2，所以 tFull 反而是\"校验通过\"的情况。\n    // 校验失败的快速路径单独测：\n    final wrongIds = [...ids];\n    wrongIds[0] = (wrongIds[0] + 1) % hanakoWordlistSize; // ${tFastFail.toStringAsFixed(2)} µs');\n    print('  这条路径占组合空间的 15/16');\n\n    // 加权平均：1/16 慢路径 + 15/16 快速失败\n    final weightedAvg = tFull / 16 + tFastFail * 15 / 16;\n    print('\\n  加权平均 (实际搜索每次):                  ${weightedAvg.toStringAsFixed(2)} µs');\n    final weightedBudget5min = 5 * 60 * 1e6 / weightedAvg;\n    final weightedBudget10min = 10 * 60 * 1e6 / weightedAvg;\n    print('  → 5 分钟实际可枚举组合:                    ${weightedBudget5min.toStringAsFixed(0)}');\n    print('  → 10 分钟实际可枚举组合:                   ${weightedBudget10min.toStringAsFixed(0)}');\n\n    // ============ K 和 D 的可行域 ├──────┼───────────┼───────────┼───────────┼───────────┤');\n    for (final k in [3, 4, 5]) {\n      final cells = <String>[];\n      for (final d in [4, 6, 8, 12]) {\n        final n = r;\n}\n\r\n","exit_code":0,"duration":{"secs":0,"nanos":409942600},"formatted_output":"","status":"completed"}

注册期：随机熵 → 12 中文名词（有序）→ 用户保存\n//   - 恢复期：12 中文名词（有序）→ 还原同一私钥\n//   - 字典与算法版本固定后，同一组词永远派生出同一私钥\n//\n// 安全说明：\n//   - mnemonic 字符串本身就是密钥的\"完整形态\"，泄漏即等同私钥泄漏；\n//   - 内存中持有 mnemonic 的时间应尽量短，用完即清；\n//   - 如需更高安全级别可加 passphrase（\"BIP-39 25th word\"），目前留空。\n\nimport 'dart:convert';\nimport 'dart:math';\nimport 'dart:typed_data';\n\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha256.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha512.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/key_derivators/pbkdf2.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/macs/hmac.dart';\nimport 'package:pointycastle/pointycastle.dart' show Pbkdf2Parameters;\n\nimport 'word_dict.dart';\n\n/// 助记词长度（固定 12 词 = 132 bit = 128 entropy + 4 checksum）。\nconst int kMnemonicLength = 12;\n\n/// 熵字节数（128 bit）。\nconst int kEntropyBytes = 16;\n\n/// 一组助记词及其派生产物。\nclass MnemonicSeed {\n  MnemonicSeed({\n    required this.words,\n    required this.entropy,\n    required this.seed,\n  });\n\n  /// 12 个中文名词（有序）。\n  final List<String> words;\n\n  /// 原始熵（16 字节）。\n  final Uint8List entropy;\n\n  /// PBKDF2 派生的 64 字节种子。\n  final Uint8List seed;\n\n  /// 取 secp256k1 私钥（seed 前 32 字节）。\n  Uint8List get privateKeyBytes => Uint8List.fromList(seed.sublist(0, 32));\n\n  /// 用空格拼接的助记词原文（PBKDF2 的输入格式）。\n  String get mnemonicString => words.join(' ');\n}\n\n/// 注册期入口：生成全新助记词与对应种子。\n///\n/// 流程：\n///   1. 调系统安全 PRNG 拉 16 字节熵；\n///   2. 计算 SHA-256 校验和的高 4 bit 拼到末尾，得到 132 bit；\n///   3. 按 11 bit 切片得到 12 个 ID，查字典得 12 个中文名词；\n///   4. PBKDF2 派生 64 字节种子。\nMnemonicSeed generateMnemonic() {\n  if (hanakoWordlistSize < kMnemonicLength) {\n    throw StateError(\n      '字典词数过少（$hanakoWordlistSize），无法生成助记词。'\n      '请扩充 word_dict.dart 至少到 $kMnemonicLength 词以上。',\n    );\n  }\n  final entropy = _secureRandom(kEntropyBytes);\n  final words = _entropyToWords(entropy);\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n}\n\n/// 恢复期入口：把已知 12 词还原成同一种子。\n///\n/// 抛 [ArgumentError]：\n///   - 词数不对；\n///   - 词不在字典里；\n///   - 校验位不对（说明用户记错了某个词）。\nMnemonicSeed mnemonicFromWords(List<String> words) {\n  if (words.length != kMnemonicLength) {\n    throw ArgumentError('助记词必须为 $kMnemonicLength 个词，收到 ${words.length}');\n  }\n  final ids = <int>[];\n  for (final w in words) {\n    final id = idByWord(w);\n    if (id == null) {\n      throw ArgumentError('词不在字典里：$w');\n    }\n    ids.add(id);\n  }\n  final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n}\n\n/// 用一组候选 ID 列表尝试恢复（恢复期 LLM 解析后用），\n/// 不抛错，校验失败返回 null，便于穷举循环外层判断。\nMnemonicSeed? tryMnemonicFromIds(List<int> ids) {\n  if (ids.length != kMnemonicLength) return null;\n  for (final id in ids) {\n    if (id < 0 || id >= hanakoWordlistSize) return null;\n  }\n  // 校验逻辑藏在 _idsToEntropy 内部，校验失败抛 [FormatException]。\n  try {\n    final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\n    final words = ids.map((i) => wordById(i)!).toList();\n    final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\n    return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\n  } on FormatException {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// entropy(16B) → 12 个 ID → 12 个词。\nList<String> _entropyToWords(Uint8List entropy) {\n  // 1. 算 4-bit 校验位（SHA-256 首字节高 4 bit）。\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(entropy);\n  final checksum = hash[0] >> 4; // 0..15\n  // 2. 把 entropy + checksum 拼成 132-bit 大整数 ↔ BigInt 操作最直观。\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\n  for (final b in entropy) {\n    bits = (bits << 8) | BigInt.from(b);\n  }\n  bits = (bits << 4) | BigInt.from(checksum);\n  // 3. 11-bit 一组切，从高到低输出 12 个 ID。\n  final ids = <int>[];\n  for (var i = kMnemonicLength - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    final shift = 11 * i;\n    final mask = BigInt.from(0x7ff); // 11 bit\n    final id = ((bits >> shift) & mask).toInt();\n    ids.add(id);\n  }\n  return ids.map((id) {\n    final w = wordById(id);\n    if (w == null) {\n      throw StateError('字典越界：ID $id（字典大小 $hanakoWordlistSize）');\n    }\n    return w;\n  }).toList();\n}\n\n/// 12 个 ID → entropy(16B)，同时校验 4-bit 校验位。\nUint8List _idsToEntropy(List<int> ids) {\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\n  for (final id in ids) {\n    bits = (bits << 11) | BigInt.from(id);\n  }\n  // 拆出末尾 4 bit 校验位。\n  final checksumExpected = (bits & BigInt.from(0xf)).toInt();\n  bits = bits >> 4;\n  // 高 128 bit 还原成 16 字节。\n  final out = Uint8List(kEntropyBytes);\n  for (var i = kEntropyBytes - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    out[i] = (bits & BigInt.from(0xff)).toInt();\n    bits = bits >> 8;\n  }\n  // 重新计算校验位比对。\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(out);\n  final checksumActual = hash[0] >> 4;\n  if (checksumExpected != checksumActual) {\n    throw const FormatException('助记词校验位不匹配（很可能某个词记错了）');\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/// PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(mnemonic, \"mnemonic\"+passphrase, 2048, 64)\n/// — BIP-39 标准 seed 派生流程。passphrase 留空。\nUint8List _mnemonicToSeed(String mnemonic, {String passphrase = ''}) {\n  final pbkdf2 = PBKDF2KeyDerivator(HMac(SHA512Digest(), 128))\n    ..init(Pbkdf2Parameters(\n      utf8.encode('mnemonic$passphrase'),\n      2048,\n      64,\n    ));\n  return pbkdf2.process(utf8.encode(mnemonic));\n}\n\nUint8List _secureRandom(int n) {\n  final rng = Random.secure();\n  final out = Uint8List(n);\n  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    out[i] = rng.nextInt(256);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\r\n","exit_code":0,"duration":{"secs":0,"nanos":391468400},"formatted_output":"","status":"completed"}

personal AI agent with memory and soul\"\npublish_to: 'none'\nversion: 600.42.0+1\n\nenvironment:\n  sdk: \">=3.10.0 <4.0.0\"\n  flutter: \">=3.27.0\"\n\ndependencies:\n  flutter:\n    sdk: flutter\n  flutter_localizations:\n    sdk: flutter\n\n  # 状态管理 & 不可变模型\n  flutter_riverpod: ^2.6.0\n  json_annotation: ^4.9.0\n\n  # ==== 网络 ====\n  dio: ^5.7.0\n  url_launcher: ^6.3.0\n  web_socket_channel: ^3.0.1\n\n  # ==== 数据库 ====\n  drift: ^2.21.0\n  sqlite3: ^2.4.0\n  sqlite3_flutter_libs: ^0.5.24\n\n  # ==== 文件 / 路径 / 配置 ====\n  path: ^1.9.0\n  path_provider: ^2.1.5\n  yaml: ^3.1.2\n  yaml_edit: ^2.2.1\n  shared_preferences: ^2.3.3\n  # flutter_secure_storage: ^9.2.2  # Windows impl 需要 ATL（VS C++/MFC 组件）。\n  #                                    Phase 1 用文件后备，Phase 4 视用户环境再启用。\n\n  # ==== UI / 渲染 ====\n  flutter_markdown: ^0.7.4\n  markdown: ^7.2.2\n  cupertino_icons: ^1.0.8\n  flutter_native_splash: ^2.4.0\n  re_editor: ^0.7.0\n  re_highlight: ^0.0.3\n  desktop_webview_window: ^0.2.3\n\n  # ==== 多窗口 & 桌面 ====\n  desktop_multi_window: ^0.2.0\n  window_manager: ^0.5.0\n  tray_manager: ^0.5.0\n  hotkey_manager: ^0.2.3\n  windows_single_instance: ^1.1.0\n\n  # ==== Headless Server / CLI ====\n  shelf: ^1.4.1\n  shelf_router: ^1.1.4\n  shelf_web_socket: ^2.0.0\n  args: ^2.5.0\n\n  # ==== 后台任务 / 调度 ====\n  cron: ^0.6.1\n\n  # ==== 加密 ====\n  crypto: ^3.0.6\n  pointycastle: ^3.9.1\n\n  # ==== Windows 原生（绕开 flutter_secure_storage 的 ATL 依赖）====\n  win32: ^5.13.0\n  ffi: ^2.1.4\n\n  # ==== 国际化 ====\n  intl: ^0.20.2\n\n  # ==== 日志 / 工具 ====\n  logger: ^2.4.0\n  collection: ^1.18.0\n  uuid: ^4.5.1\n  meta: ^1.16.0\n\ndev_dependencies:\n  flutter_test:\n    sdk: flutter\n  flutter_lints: ^6.0.0\n  build_runner: ^2.4.13\n  json_serializable: ^6.8.0\n  drift_dev: ^2.21.0\n\nflutter:\n  uses-material-design: true\n  generate: true\n\r\n","exit_code":0,"duration":{"secs":0,"nanos":391961800},"formatted_output":"","status":"completed"}
[2026-04-30 11:10:32] Codex: 天使，可以外挂，而且我建议先不要直接冲 GPU，先做 **Rust/C++ 原生插件 + 批处理枚举**，收益更稳。\n\n当前 10 分钟约 75,000 组的瓶颈不是“汉明距离矩阵枚举”，而是校验通过后的慢路径：\n\n- 15/16 的组合会被 BIP-39 checksum 快速淘汰，约几微秒\n- 1/16 的组合会进入 PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512 2048 轮\n- PBKDF2 才是主要耗时，当前 Dart/PointyCastle 下约 127ms/次完整尝试\n- secp256k1 公钥派生也有成本，但小于 PBKDF2\n- SHA-256 / Set.contains 基本不是问题\n\n所以 GPU 不是用来加速“汉明距离”，而是用来批量加速 **PBKDF2 + 可能的公钥派生**。\n\n**大概能提升多少**\n\n以当前 `75,000/10分钟` 为 1x 估算：\n\n| 候选宽度 | 全矩阵规模 | 当前耗时 | 10x | 100x | 1000x |\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n| K=3 | 531,441 | 约 70 分钟 | 约 7 分钟 | 约 42 秒 | 几秒级 |\n| K=4 | 16,777,216 | 约 37 小时 | 约 3.7 小时 | 约 22 分钟 | 约 2.2 分钟 |\n| K=5 | 244,140,625 | 约 22.5 天 | 约 2.25 天 | 约 5.4 小时 | 约 32 分钟 |\n\n也就是说：\n\n- **K=3 全矩阵算尽很现实**，甚至 native CPU 多核就可能够用。\n- **K=4 用 GPU 后有希望进入可接受范围**，但取决于显卡、驱动和实现。\n- **K=5 全算尽不适合作为默认目标**，即使用高端 GPU 也可能带来发热、功耗、风扇、卡顿和安全风险。\n\n**Flutter 里怎么做**\n\n可以，推荐方案是：\n\n1. Flutter 保留 UI、进度、取消、任务调度。\n2. 恢复核心做成 native 插件。\n3. 用 Dart FFI 调 Rust/C/C++。\n4. 不要每个候选都跨 FFI 调一次，而是一次传入候选矩阵、目标 hash、预算、线程数。\n5. native 内部批量枚举、checksum 过滤、PBKDF2、公钥派生、hash 对比。\n6. 只把进度和命中结果回传给 Flutter。\n\n我更推荐 Rust：\n\n- 跨 Windows/macOS/Linux 比较稳\n- Rayon 多线程好做\n- crypto / secp256k1 库成熟\n- 可以先做 CPU 后端，再扩 GPU 后端\n- Flutter 端通过 FFI 统一调用\n\nGPU 可以作为第二阶段：\n\n- Windows/Linux：CUDA / OpenCL / Vulkan compute\n- macOS：Metal\n- Flutter 不直接承担 GPU compute，只通过 FFI 调 native 动态库\n- 初版可以只支持 Windows NVIDIA CUDA 或 Vulkan/OpenCL 实验模式\n\n**我的判断**\n\nGPU 会显著提高 benchmark，但“全矩阵算尽”不是一个统一答案，取决于 K。比较务实的目标是：\n\n- 默认模式：Dart fallback\n- 高性能模式：Rust native CPU\n- 极限模式：GPU，可选启用\n- K=3：争取全矩阵\n- K=4：GPU 模式争取全矩阵\n- K=5：仍以汉明距离预算搜索为主，不默认全扫\n\n另外要注意安全：恢复能力越强，攻击者离线枚举能力也越强。后续服务端恢复接口必须有节流、设备绑定、账户级限频，不能让目标 hash 集合被无限查询。
[2026-04-30 11:14:05] 天使: 攻击者只要确定我们的密钥算法，就能自行编写工具进行恢复。\n\n本质上，我们不能依靠算法的保密性来确保安全性。这套算法在加密钱包中已有应用，即助记词系统，只不过我们针对助记词系统设计了一套容错恢复机制。虽然我可以限制前端的最大容错量来确保安全，但在速度上，还是需要尽可能优化到位。\n\n我希望你拉一个基准测试（Benchmark）：\n1. 环境要求：在 Windows 环境下运行。\n2. 硬件参考：直接利用我电脑现有的显卡进行计算。\n3. 测试时长：做一个 1 分钟或 10 分钟的基准。\n\n这样做的目的是确定我们最高的恢复速度。如果攻击者逆向了我们的客户端，掌握了内部的恢复逻辑并编写出更高效的恢复工具，我们必须明确：在拿到 LLM 给出的矩阵后，我们能否以极快的速度完成计算。\n\n无论是对于我们业务层面上的容错处理，还是模拟攻击者尝试破解，这个基准测试都非常有帮助。所以，我现在需要你去做一个利用显卡计算的基准测试。
[2026-04-30 11:14:22] Codex: 天使，我先按“Windows 原生 + 当前显卡”来做，不走 WSL。接下来我会先确认显卡型号、驱动和本机可用的 GPU 计算栈，然后选最小可验证路径拉一个真实 benchmark。

token count: 2428\nOutput:\n// lib/identity/recovery.dart\r\n//\r\n// 私钥恢复器（v2 矩阵搜索版）。\r\n//\r\n// 与 v1 全排列穷举的根本区别：\r\n//   v1: LLM 给出扁平 ID 列表，客户端尝试该列表的所有排列（最坏 12!）\r\n//   v2: LLM 给出 12 列 × K 行的候选矩阵（按故事顺序），客户端按\r\n//       \"汉明距离 d 递增\" 枚举：d=0 → 每列取 rank-0；d=k → 替换 k 列\r\n//       为 rank-1..rank-(K-1)。最坏 K^12 次。\r\n//\r\n// 与 MVP §10.5 + 用户 2026-04-27 设计对齐。\r\n//\r\n// 性能预算（基于 tools/recovery_benchmark.dart 实测，dart run JIT 模式）：\r\n//   - 单次完整 PBKDF2 + secp256k1 派生 + 公钥哈希比对 ≈ 86 ms\r\n//   - 校验位过滤后加权平均 ≈ 5.4 ms / 次\r\n//   - 单线程 5 分钟可枚举 ≈ 55,000 次\r\n//   - 4 核并行 + AOT (~2x) 估算 5 分钟 ≈ 400,000 次\r\n//\r\n// 参数 (与 story_parser.kStoryParserCandidatesPerColumn = 3 对齐)：\r\n//   K = 3            每列 top-3 候选\r\n//   D_target = 8     5 分钟软目标（容错 8 词）\r\n//   D_max = 12       10 分钟硬上限（K=3 全空间 = 531,441）\r\n//\r\n// Isolate 并行：\r\n//   把\"d 层枚举\"按 (combo_index mod N_workers) 分给 N_workers 个 isolate；\r\n//   每个 worker 独立做 PBKDF2 + secp256k1，找到匹配立刻通知主 isolate\r\n//   终止其他 worker。\r\n\r\nimport 'dart:async';\r\nimport 'dart:io';\r\nimport 'dart:isolate';\r\nimport 'dart:typed_data';\r\n\r\nimport 'keypair.dart';\r\nimport 'mnemonic.dart';\r\n\r\n/// 公钥比对回调。返回 true 表示该公钥在服务端 / 本地缓存里已注册。\r\n///\r\n/// 注意：此回调会在主 isolate 调用——worker isolate 找到候选后把结果传回\r\n/// 主 isolate 由这里做最终比对（避免 checker 闭包跨 isolate 序列化问题）。\r\ntypedef PublicKeyChecker = Future<bool> Function(String publicKeyHexUncompressed);\r\n\r\nclass RecoveryOutcome {\r\n  RecoveryOutcome.success(this.seed, {required this.attempted, required this.elapsedMs})\r\n      : failed = false,\r\n        timedOut = false;\r\n  RecoveryOutcome.failed({\r\n    required this.attempted,\r\n    required this.timedOut,\r\n    required this.elapsedMs,\r\n  })  : seed = null,\r\n        failed = true;\r\n\r\n  final MnemonicSeed? seed;\r\n  final int attempted;\r\n  final int elapsedMs;\r\n  final bool failed;\r\n  final bool timedOut;\r\n\r\n  bool get success => seed != null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nclass Recovery {\r\n  Recovery({\r\n    required this.checker,\r\n    this.kPerColumn = 3,\r\n    this.dMaxSoft = 8,\r\n    this.dMaxHard = 12,\r\n    this.softDeadline = const Duration(minutes: 5),\r\n    this.hardDeadline = const Duration(minutes: 10),\r\n    this.workerCount,\r\n    this.onProgress,\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  /// 服务端 / 本地公钥比对器。\r\n  final PublicKeyChecker checker;\r\n\r\n  /// 每列候选数。必须与 LLM 输出的矩阵列数一致。\r\n  final int kPerColumn;\r\n\r\n  /// 软目标汉明距离上限。在软超时前优先搜到这个深度。\r\n  final int dMaxSoft;\r\n\r\n  /// 硬上限。超过这个深度认定不可能恢复。\r\n  final int dMaxHard;\r\n\r\n  /// 软超时（达到后切换为\"减速 + 询问用户是否继续\"模式，但仍继续搜直到硬超时）。\r\n  final Duration softDeadline;\r\n\r\n  /// 硬超时。超过即返回失败。\r\n  final Duration hardDeadline;\r\n\r\n  /// 并行 worker 数。null 时自动取 max(1, processors - 1)。\r\n  final int? workerCount;\r\n\r\n  /// 进度回调（用于 UI 显示\"已尝试 X 次 / 已用时 Y 秒\"）。\r\n  final void Function(RecoveryProgress progress)? onProgress;\r\n\r\n  /// 主入口：[matrix] 是 12 行 × K 列的候选 ID 矩阵（外层 = 故事意象顺序）。\r\n  Future<RecoveryOutcome> tryRecover(List<List<int>> matrix) async {\r\n    // 输入合法性\r\n    if (matrix.length != kMnemonicLength) {\r\n      return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n        attempted: 0,\r\n        timedOut: false,\r\n        elapsedMs: 0,\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n    for (final col in matrix) {\r\n      if (col.length != kPerColumn) {\r\n        return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n          attempted: 0,\r\n          timedOut: false,\r\n          elapsedMs: 0,\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();\r\n    final nWorkers = workerCount ?? _defaultWorkerCount();\r\n    final attemptedCounter = _AtomicCounter();\r\n\r\n    // 已知公钥缓存：如果 checker 命中过某些公钥，可缓存避免重复网络调用。\r\n    // 不过当前 checker 一般是常数时间（本地比对或服务端批查），所以暂不加缓存。\r\n\r\n    // 把矩阵转成扁平形式让 worker 用：matrixFlat[col * K + rank] = id\r\n    final flat = Int32List(kMnemonicLength * kPerColumn);\r\n    for (var c = 0; c < kMnemonicLength; c++) {\r\n      for (var r = 0; r < kPerColumn; r++) {\r\n        flat[c * kPerColumn + r] = matrix[c][r];\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // 按汉明距离 d=0..dMaxHard 递增搜索。每个 d 层的所有组合并行分配给 workers。\r\n    for (var d = 0; d <= dMaxHard; d++) {\r\n      // 软超时检查：到了软超时但还在搜 d <= dMaxSoft 范围内时仍继续；\r\n      // 超出软目标后只在 dMaxSoft < d <= dMaxHard 时才搜。\r\n      final elapsed = sw.elapsedMilliseconds;\r\n      if (elapsed > hardDeadline.inMilliseconds) {\r\n        return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n          attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n          timedOut: true,\r\n          elapsedMs: elapsed,\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n      // 进度通知\r\n      onProgress?.call(RecoveryProgress(\r\n        currentHammingDistance: d,\r\n        attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n        elapsedMs: elapsed,\r\n      ));\r\n\r\n      final hit = await _searchAtDistance(\r\n        flat: flat,\r\n        d: d,\r\n        nWorkers: nWorkers,\r\n        attemptedCounter: attemptedCounter,\r\n        deadline: }\r\n\r\n  /// 在汉明距离 d 层搜索，把所有组合分给 nWorkers 个 worker isolate。\r\n  ///\r\n  /// 每个 worker 收到自己的分片范围（combo_index 区间），独立枚举 + 检查\r\n  /// 校验位 + PBKDF2 + secp256k1，找到匹配回传种子。任一 worker 命中后\r\n  /// 取消其他 worker。\r\n  Future<MnemonicSeed?> _searchAtDistance({\r\n    required Int32List flat,\r\n    required int d,\r\n    required int nWorkers,\r\n    required _AtomicCounter attemptedCounter,\r\n    required Duration deadline,\r\n    required Stopwatch startedAt,\r\n  }) async {\r\n    // d 层的总组合数 = C(12, d) * (K-1)^d\r\n    // C(12,0) = 1, C(12,12) = 1, C(12,6) = 924\r\n    final cIdxMax = 注册期：随机熵 → 12 中文名词（有序）→ 用户保存\r\n//   - 恢复期：12 中文名词（有序）→ 还原同一私钥\r\n//   - 字典与算法版本固定后，同一组词永远派生出同一私钥\r\n//\r\n// 安全说明：\r\n//   - mnemonic 字符串本身就是密钥的\"完整形态\"，泄漏即等同私钥泄漏；\r\n//   - 内存中持有 mnemonic 的时间应尽量短，用完即清；\r\n//   - 如需更高安全级别可加 passphrase（\"BIP-39 25th word\"），目前留空。\r\n\r\nimport 'dart:convert';\r\nimport 'dart:math';\r\nimport 'dart:typed_data';\r\n\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha256.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha512.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/key_derivators/pbkdf2.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/macs/hmac.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/pointycastle.dart' show Pbkdf2Parameters;\r\n\r\nimport 'word_dict.dart';\r\n\r\n/// 助记词长度（固定 12 词 = 132 bit = 128 entropy + 4 checksum）。\r\nconst int kMnemonicLength = 12;\r\n\r\n/// 熵字节数（128 bit）。\r\nconst int kEntropyBytes = 16;\r\n\r\n/// 一组助记词及其派生产物。\r\nclass MnemonicSeed {\r\n  MnemonicSeed({\r\n    required this.words,\r\n    required this.entropy,\r\n    required this.seed,\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  /// 12 个中文名词（有序）。\r\n  final List<String> words;\r\n\r\n  /// 原始熵（16 字节）。\r\n  final Uint8List entropy;\r\n\r\n  /// PBKDF2 派生的 64 字节种子。\r\n  final Uint8List seed;\r\n\r\n  /// 取 secp256k1 私钥（seed 前 32 字节）。\r\n  Uint8List get privateKeyBytes => Uint8List.fromList(seed.sublist(0, 32));\r\n\r\n  /// 用空格拼接的助记词原文（PBKDF2 的输入格式）。\r\n  String get mnemonicString => words.join(' ');\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 注册期入口：生成全新助记词与对应种子。\r\n///\r\n/// 流程：\r\n///   1. 调系统安全 PRNG 拉 16 字节熵；\r\n///   2. 计算 SHA-256 校验和的高 4 bit 拼到末尾，得到 132 bit；\r\n///   3. 按 11 bit 切片得到 12 个 ID，查字典得 12 个中文名词；\r\n///   4. PBKDF2 派生 64 字节种子。\r\nMnemonicSeed generateMnemonic() {\r\n  if (hanakoWordlistSize < kMnemonicLength) {\r\n    throw StateError(\r\n      '字典词数过少（$hanakoWordlistSize），无法生成助记词。'\r\n      '请扩充 word_dict.dart 至少到 $kMnemonicLength 词以上。',\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  final entropy = _secureRandom(kEntropyBytes);\r\n  final words = _entropyToWords(entropy);\r\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 恢复期入口：把已知 12 词还原成同一种子。\r\n///\r\n/// 抛 [ArgumentError]：\r\n///   - 词数不对；\r\n///   - 词不在字典里；\r\n///   - 校验位不对（说明用户记错了某个词）。\r\nMnemonicSeed mnemonicFromWords(List<String> words) {\r\n  if (words.length != kMnemonicLength) {\r\n    throw ArgumentError('助记词必须为 $kMnemonicLength 个词，收到 ${words.length}');\r\n  }\r\n  final ids = <int>[];\r\n  for (final w in words) {\r\n    final id = idByWord(w);\r\n    if (id == null) {\r\n      throw ArgumentError('词不在字典里：$w');\r\n    }\r\n    ids.add(id);\r\n  }\r\n  final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\r\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 用一组候选 ID 列表尝试恢复（恢复期 LLM 解析后用），\r\n/// 不抛错，校验失败返回 null，便于穷举循环外层判断。\r\nMnemonicSeed? tryMnemonicFromIds(List<int> ids) {\r\n  if (ids.length != kMnemonicLength) return null;\r\n  for (final id in ids) {\r\n    if (id < 0 || id >= hanakoWordlistSize) return null;\r\n  }\r\n  // 校验逻辑藏在 _idsToEntropy 内部，校验失败抛 [FormatException]。\r\n  try {\r\n    final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\r\n    final words = ids.map((i) => wordById(i)!).toList();\r\n    final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n    return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n  } on FormatException {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n/// entropy(16B) → 12 个 ID → 12 个词。\r\nList<String> _entropyToWords(Uint8List entropy) {\r\n  // 1. 算 4-bit 校验位（SHA-256 首字节高 4 bit）。\r\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(entropy);\r\n  final checksum = hash[0] >> 4; // 0..15\r\n  // 2. 把 entropy + checksum 拼成 132-bit 大整数 ↔ BigInt 操作最直观。\r\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\r\n  for (final b in entropy) {\r\n    bits = (bits << 8) | BigInt.from(b);\r\n  }\r\n  bits = (bits << 4) | BigInt.from(checksum);\r\n  // 3. 11-bit 一组切，从高到低输出 12 个 ID。\r\n  final ids = <int>[];\r\n  for (var i = kMnemonicLength - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    final shift = 11 * i;\r\n    final mask = BigInt.from(0x7ff); // 11 bit\r\n    final id = ((bits >> shift) & mask).toInt();\r\n    ids.add(id);\r\n  }\r\n  return ids.map((id) {\r\n    final w = wordById(id);\r\n    if (w == null) {\r\n      throw StateError('字典越界：ID $id（字典大小 $hanakoWordlistSize）');\r\n    }\r\n    return w;\r\n  }).toList();\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 12 个 ID → entropy(16B)，同时校验 4-bit 校验位。\r\nUint8List _idsToEntropy(List<int> ids) {\r\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\r\n  for (final id in ids) {\r\n    bits = (bits << 11) | BigInt.from(id);\r\n  }\r\n  // 拆出末尾 4 bit 校验位。\r\n  final checksumExpected = (bits & BigInt.from(0xf)).toInt();\r\n  bits = bits >> 4;\r\n  // 高 128 bit 还原成 16 字节。\r\n  final out = Uint8List(kEntropyBytes);\r\n  for (var i = kEntropyBytes - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    out[i] = (bits & BigInt.from(0xff)).toInt();\r\n    bits = bits >> 8;\r\n  }\r\n  // 重新计算校验位比对。\r\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(out);\r\n  final checksumActual = hash[0] >> 4;\r\n  if (checksumExpected != checksumActual) {\r\n    throw const FormatException('助记词校验位不匹配（很可能某个词记错了）');\r\n  }\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(mnemonic, \"mnemonic\"+passphrase, 2048, 64)\r\n/// — BIP-39 标准 seed 派生流程。passphrase 留空。\r\nUint8List _mnemonicToSeed(String mnemonic, {String passphrase = ''}) {\r\n  final pbkdf2 = PBKDF2KeyDerivator(HMac(SHA512Digest(), 128))\r\n    ..init(Pbkdf2Parameters(\r\n      utf8.encode('mnemonic$passphrase'),\r\n      2048,\r\n      64,\r\n    ));\r\n  return pbkdf2.process(utf8.encode(mnemonic));\r\n}\r\n\r\nUint8List _secureRandom(int n) {\r\n  final rng = Random.secure();\r\n  final out = Uint8List(n);\r\n  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n    out[i] = rng.nextInt(256);\r\n  }\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n"}

= _searchSpace(12, k, d);\r\n        cells.add(n.toString().padLeft(9));\r\n      }\r\n      print('  │ K=$k  │ ${cells.join(' │ ')} │');\r\n    }\r\n    print('  └──────┴───────────┴───────────┴───────────┴───────────┘');\r\n    print('  5 分钟可承受上限:  ${weightedBudget5min.toStringAsFixed(0)}');\r\n    print('  10 分钟可承受上限: ${weightedBudget10min.toStringAsFixed(0)}');\r\n\r\n    // 验证：K=3 D=12 (理论最大) 在预算内吗？\r\n    final maxSpaceK3 = _searchSpace(12, 3, 12);\r\n    final maxSpaceK4 = _searchSpace(12, 4, 12);\r\n    final maxSpaceK5 = _searchSpace(12, 5, 12);\r\n    print('\\n  全空间 K=3 D=12: $maxSpaceK3   (= 3^12)');\r\n    print('  全空间 K=4 D=12: $maxSpaceK4   (= 4^12)');\r\n    print('  全空间 K=5 D=12: $maxSpaceK5   (= 5^12)');\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nint _searchSpace(int cols, int k, int dMax) {\r\n  // Σ_{d=0..dMax} C(cols,d) · (k-1)^d\r\n  var total = 0;\r\n  for (var d = 0; d <= dMax; d++) {\r\n    total += _binom(cols, d) * _pow((k - 1), d);\r\n  }\r\n  return total;\r\n}\r\n\r\nint _binom(int n, int k) {\r\n  if (k < 0 || k > n) return 0;\r\n  var r = 1;\r\n  for (var i = 0; i < k; i++) {\r\n    r = r * (n - i) ~/ (i + 1);\r\n  }\r\n  return r;\r\n}\r\n\r\nint _pow(int base, int exp) {\r\n  var r = 1;\r\n  for (var i = 0; i < exp; i++) {\r\n    r *= base;\r\n  }\r\n  return r;\r\n}\r\n"}

注册期：随机熵 → 12 中文名词（有序）→ 用户保存\r\n//   - 恢复期：12 中文名词（有序）→ 还原同一私钥\r\n//   - 字典与算法版本固定后，同一组词永远派生出同一私钥\r\n//\r\n// 安全说明：\r\n//   - mnemonic 字符串本身就是密钥的\"完整形态\"，泄漏即等同私钥泄漏；\r\n//   - 内存中持有 mnemonic 的时间应尽量短，用完即清；\r\n//   - 如需更高安全级别可加 passphrase（\"BIP-39 25th word\"），目前留空。\r\n\r\nimport 'dart:convert';\r\nimport 'dart:math';\r\nimport 'dart:typed_data';\r\n\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha256.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/digests/sha512.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/key_derivators/pbkdf2.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/macs/hmac.dart';\r\nimport 'package:pointycastle/pointycastle.dart' show Pbkdf2Parameters;\r\n\r\nimport 'word_dict.dart';\r\n\r\n/// 助记词长度（固定 12 词 = 132 bit = 128 entropy + 4 checksum）。\r\nconst int kMnemonicLength = 12;\r\n\r\n/// 熵字节数（128 bit）。\r\nconst int kEntropyBytes = 16;\r\n\r\n/// 一组助记词及其派生产物。\r\nclass MnemonicSeed {\r\n  MnemonicSeed({\r\n    required this.words,\r\n    required this.entropy,\r\n    required this.seed,\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  /// 12 个中文名词（有序）。\r\n  final List<String> words;\r\n\r\n  /// 原始熵（16 字节）。\r\n  final Uint8List entropy;\r\n\r\n  /// PBKDF2 派生的 64 字节种子。\r\n  final Uint8List seed;\r\n\r\n  /// 取 secp256k1 私钥（seed 前 32 字节）。\r\n  Uint8List get privateKeyBytes => Uint8List.fromList(seed.sublist(0, 32));\r\n\r\n  /// 用空格拼接的助记词原文（PBKDF2 的输入格式）。\r\n  String get mnemonicString => words.join(' ');\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 注册期入口：生成全新助记词与对应种子。\r\n///\r\n/// 流程：\r\n///   1. 调系统安全 PRNG 拉 16 字节熵；\r\n///   2. 计算 SHA-256 校验和的高 4 bit 拼到末尾，得到 132 bit；\r\n///   3. 按 11 bit 切片得到 12 个 ID，查字典得 12 个中文名词；\r\n///   4. PBKDF2 派生 64 字节种子。\r\nMnemonicSeed generateMnemonic() {\r\n  if (hanakoWordlistSize < kMnemonicLength) {\r\n    throw StateError(\r\n      '字典词数过少（$hanakoWordlistSize），无法生成助记词。'\r\n      '请扩充 word_dict.dart 至少到 $kMnemonicLength 词以上。',\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n  final entropy = _secureRandom(kEntropyBytes);\r\n  final words = _entropyToWords(entropy);\r\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 恢复期入口：把已知 12 词还原成同一种子。\r\n///\r\n/// 抛 [ArgumentError]：\r\n///   - 词数不对；\r\n///   - 词不在字典里；\r\n///   - 校验位不对（说明用户记错了某个词）。\r\nMnemonicSeed mnemonicFromWords(List<String> words) {\r\n  if (words.length != kMnemonicLength) {\r\n    throw ArgumentError('助记词必须为 $kMnemonicLength 个词，收到 ${words.length}');\r\n  }\r\n  final ids = <int>[];\r\n  for (final w in words) {\r\n    final id = idByWord(w);\r\n    if (id == null) {\r\n      throw ArgumentError('词不在字典里：$w');\r\n    }\r\n    ids.add(id);\r\n  }\r\n  final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\r\n  final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n  return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 用一组候选 ID 列表尝试恢复（恢复期 LLM 解析后用），\r\n/// 不抛错，校验失败返回 null，便于穷举循环外层判断。\r\nMnemonicSeed? tryMnemonicFromIds(List<int> ids) {\r\n  if (ids.length != kMnemonicLength) return null;\r\n  for (final id in ids) {\r\n    if (id < 0 || id >= hanakoWordlistSize) return null;\r\n  }\r\n  // 校验逻辑藏在 _idsToEntropy 内部，校验失败抛 [FormatException]。\r\n  try {\r\n    final entropy = _idsToEntropy(ids);\r\n    final words = ids.map((i) => wordById(i)!).toList();\r\n    final seed = _mnemonicToSeed(words.join(' '));\r\n    return MnemonicSeed(words: words, entropy: entropy, seed: seed);\r\n  } on FormatException {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n\r\n/// entropy(16B) → 12 个 ID → 12 个词。\r\nList<String> _entropyToWords(Uint8List entropy) {\r\n  // 1. 算 4-bit 校验位（SHA-256 首字节高 4 bit）。\r\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(entropy);\r\n  final checksum = hash[0] >> 4; // 0..15\r\n  // 2. 把 entropy + checksum 拼成 132-bit 大整数 ↔ BigInt 操作最直观。\r\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\r\n  for (final b in entropy) {\r\n    bits = (bits << 8) | BigInt.from(b);\r\n  }\r\n  bits = (bits << 4) | BigInt.from(checksum);\r\n  // 3. 11-bit 一组切，从高到低输出 12 个 ID。\r\n  final ids = <int>[];\r\n  for (var i = kMnemonicLength - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    final shift = 11 * i;\r\n    final mask = BigInt.from(0x7ff); // 11 bit\r\n    final id = ((bits >> shift) & mask).toInt();\r\n    ids.add(id);\r\n  }\r\n  return ids.map((id) {\r\n    final w = wordById(id);\r\n    if (w == null) {\r\n      throw StateError('字典越界：ID $id（字典大小 $hanakoWordlistSize）');\r\n    }\r\n    return w;\r\n  }).toList();\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// 12 个 ID → entropy(16B)，同时校验 4-bit 校验位。\r\nUint8List _idsToEntropy(List<int> ids) {\r\n  var bits = BigInt.zero;\r\n  for (final id in ids) {\r\n    bits = (bits << 11) | BigInt.from(id);\r\n  }\r\n  // 拆出末尾 4 bit 校验位。\r\n  final checksumExpected = (bits & BigInt.from(0xf)).toInt();\r\n  bits = bits >> 4;\r\n  // 高 128 bit 还原成 16 字节。\r\n  final out = Uint8List(kEntropyBytes);\r\n  for (var i = kEntropyBytes - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    out[i] = (bits & BigInt.from(0xff)).toInt();\r\n    bits = bits >> 8;\r\n  }\r\n  // 重新计算校验位比对。\r\n  final hash = SHA256Digest().process(out);\r\n  final checksumActual = hash[0] >> 4;\r\n  if (checksumExpected != checksumActual) {\r\n    throw const FormatException('助记词校验位不匹配（很可能某个词记错了）');\r\n  }\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/// PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(mnemonic, \"mnemonic\"+passphrase, 2048, 64)\r\n/// — BIP-39 标准 seed 派生流程。passphrase 留空。\r\nUint8List _mnemonicToSeed(String mnemonic, {String passphrase = ''}) {\r\n  final pbkdf2 = PBKDF2KeyDerivator(HMac(SHA512Digest(), 128))\r\n    ..init(Pbkdf2Parameters(\r\n      utf8.encode('mnemonic$passphrase'),\r\n      2048,\r\n      64,\r\n    ));\r\n  return pbkdf2.process(utf8.encode(mnemonic));\r\n}\r\n\r\nUint8List _secureRandom(int n) {\r\n  final rng = Random.secure();\r\n  final out = Uint8List(n);\r\n  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\r\n    out[i] = rng.nextInt(256);\r\n  }\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n","exit_code":0,"duration":{"secs":0,"nanos":446596600},"formatted_output":"","status":"completed"}

用户 2026-04-27 设计对齐。\r\n//\r\n// 性能预算（基于 tools/recovery_benchmark.dart 实测，dart run JIT 模式）：\r\n//   - 单次完整 PBKDF2 + secp256k1 派生 + 公钥哈希比对 ≈ 86 ms\r\n//   - 校验位过滤后加权平均 ≈ 5.4 ms / 次\r\n//   - 单线程 5 分钟可枚举 'mnemonic.dart';\r\n\r\n/// 公钥比对回调。返回 true 表示该公钥在服务端 / 本地缓存里已注册。\r\n///\r\n/// 注意：此回调会在主 isolate 调用——worker isolate 找到候选后把结果传回\r\n/// 主 isolate 由这里做最终比对（避免 checker 闭包跨 isolate 序列化问题）。\r\ntypedef timedOut;\r\n\r\n  bool get success => seed != null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nclass Recovery {\r\n  Recovery({\r\n    required this.checker,\r\n    this.kPerColumn = 3,\r\n    this.dMaxSoft = 8,\r\n    this.dMaxHard = 12,\r\n    this.softDeadline = const Duration(minutes: 5),\r\n    this.hardDeadline = const Duration(minutes: 10),\r\n    this.workerCount,\r\n    this.onProgress,\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  /// 服务端 / 本地公钥比对器。\r\n  final PublicKeyChecker checker;\r\n\r\n  /// 每列候选数。必须与 LLM 输出的矩阵列数一致。\r\n  final int kPerColumn;\r\n\r\n  /// 软目标汉明距离上限。在软超时前优先搜到这个深度。\r\n  final int dMaxSoft;\r\n\r\n  /// 硬上限。超过这个深度认定不可能恢复。\r\n  final int dMaxHard;\r\n\r\n  /// 软超时（达到后切换为\"减速 + 询问用户是否继续\"模式，但仍继续搜直到硬超时）。\r\n  final Duration softDeadline;\r\n\r\n  /// 硬超时。超过即返回失败。\r\n  final Duration hardDeadline;\r\n\r\n  /// 并行 worker 数。null 时自动取 max(1, processors - 1)。\r\n  final int? workerCount;\r\n\r\n  /// 进度回调（用于 UI 显示\"已尝试 X 次 / 已用时 Y 秒\"）。\r\n  final void Function(RecoveryProgress progress)? onProgress;\r\n\r\n  /// 主入口：[matrix] 是 12 行 × K 列的候选 ID 矩阵（外层 = 故事意象顺序）。\r\n  Future<RecoveryOutcome> tryRecover(List<List<int>> matrix) async {\r\n    // 输入合法性\r\n    if (matrix.length != kMnemonicLength) {\r\n      return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n        attempted: 0,\r\n        timedOut: false,\r\n        elapsedMs: 0,\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n    for (final col in matrix) {\r\n      if (col.length != kPerColumn) {\r\n        return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n          attempted: 0,\r\n          timedOut: false,\r\n          elapsedMs: 0,\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();\r\n    final nWorkers = workerCount ?? _defaultWorkerCount();\r\n    final attemptedCounter = _AtomicCounter();\r\n\r\n    // 已知公钥缓存：如果 checker 命中过某些公钥，可缓存避免重复网络调用。\r\n    // 不过当前 checker 一般是常数时间（本地比对或服务端批查），所以暂不加缓存。\r\n\r\n    // 把矩阵转成扁平形式让 worker 用：matrixFlat[col * K + rank] = id\r\n    final flat = Int32List(kMnemonicLength * kPerColumn);\r\n    for (var c = 0; c < kMnemonicLength; c++) {\r\n      for (var r = 0; r < kPerColumn; r++) {\r\n        flat[c * kPerColumn + r] = matrix[c][r];\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // 按汉明距离 d=0..dMaxHard 递增搜索。每个 d 层的所有组合并行分配给 workers。\r\n    for (var d = 0; d <= dMaxHard; d++) {\r\n      // 软超时检查：到了软超时但还在搜 d <= dMaxSoft 范围内时仍继续；\r\n      // 超出软目标后只在 dMaxSoft < d <= dMaxHard 时才搜。\r\n      final elapsed = sw.elapsedMilliseconds;\r\n      if (elapsed > hardDeadline.inMilliseconds) {\r\n        return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n          attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n          timedOut: true,\r\n          elapsedMs: elapsed,\r\n        );\r\n      }\r\n      // 进度通知\r\n      onProgress?.call(RecoveryProgress(\r\n        currentHammingDistance: d,\r\n        attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n        elapsedMs: elapsed,\r\n      ));\r\n\r\n      final hit = await _searchAtDistance(\r\n        flat: flat,\r\n        d: d,\r\n        nWorkers: nWorkers,\r\n        attemptedCounter: attemptedCounter,\r\n        deadline: hardDeadline,\r\n        startedAt: sw,\r\n      );\r\n      if (hit != null) {\r\n        // 找到候选种子，主 isolate 用 checker 二次确认（防止 worker 假阳性）\r\n        final pair = HanakoKeyPair.fromPrivateKeyBytes(hit.privateKeyBytes);\r\n        if (await checker(pair.publicKeyHex)) {\r\n          return RecoveryOutcome.success(\r\n            hit,\r\n            attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n            elapsedMs: sw.elapsedMilliseconds,\r\n          );\r\n        }\r\n        // 假阳性（不太可能，但防一手）：继续搜\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return RecoveryOutcome.failed(\r\n      attempted: attemptedCounter.value,\r\n      timedOut: sw.elapsedMilliseconds >= hardDeadline.inMilliseconds,\r\n      elapsedMs: sw.elapsedMilliseconds,\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  /// 在汉明距离 d 层搜索，把所有组合分给 nWorkers 个 worker isolate。\r\n  ///\r\n  /// 每个 worker 收到自己的分片范围（combo_index 区间），独立枚举 + 检查\r\n  /// 校验位 + PBKDF2 + secp256k1，找到匹配回传种子。任一 worker 命中后\r\n  /// 取消其他 worker。\r\n  Future<MnemonicSeed?> _searchAtDistance({\r\n    required Int32List flat,\r\n    required int d,\r\n    required int nWorkers,\r\n    required _AtomicCounter attemptedCounter,\r\n    required Duration deadline,\r\n    required Stopwatch startedAt,\r\n  }) async {\r\n    // d 层的总组合数 = C(12, d) * (K-1)^d\r\n    // C(12,0) = 1, C(12,12) = 1, C(12,6) = 924\r\n    final cIdxMax = _binom(kMnemonicLength, d);\r\n    final substMax = _pow(kPerColumn - 1, d);\r\n    final totalCombos = cIdxMax * token count: 1176\nOutput:\n---\r\nname: uv-project-management\r\ndescription: 使用 uv 管理 Python 项目的完整工作流，包括安装 uv、用 uv 安装 Python、用 uv init 初始化项目、用 uv add 和 uv remove 维护依赖、用 uv sync 和 uv run 运行项目，以及把基于 requirements.txt、pip 或 venv 的旧项目整体迁移到 pyproject.toml 和 uv.lock 驱动的纯 uv 模式。当用户想编写 uv 运行指南、统一 Python 环境管理、把旧项目切到 uv、或排查 uv 常见使用问题时使用此技能。\r\n---\r\n\r\n# uv Project Management\r\n\r\n让 Python 项目的安装、运行、依赖维护和迁移都统一走 `uv`。\r\n\r\n## 核心原则\r\n\r\n1. 把 `uv` 当成唯一入口。\r\n2. 把 `pyproject.toml` 当成依赖声明源。\r\n3. 把 `uv.lock` 当成可复现环境的锁定结果。\r\n4. 让 `uv sync` 和 `uv run` 成为日常运行入口。\r\n5. 旧项目完成迁移后，不再把 `requirements.txt` 当主维护文件。\r\n\r\n## 先判断项目状态\r\n\r\n先在目标 Python 项目根目录确认当前属于哪一种：\r\n\r\n- 已有 `pyproject.toml` 和 `uv.lock`\r\n- 已有 `pyproject.toml`，但依赖仍主要写在 `requirements.txt`\r\n- 只有 `requirements.txt`，还没进入 `uv` 项目模式\r\n- 完全新项目，还没初始化\r\n\r\n按状态走下面的流程，不要混着用。\r\n\r\n## 一条龙运行流程\r\n\r\n### 1. 安装 uv\r\n\r\nWindows PowerShell：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\npowershell -ExecutionPolicy ByPass -c \"irm https://astral.sh/uv/install.ps1 | iex\"\r\n```\r\n\r\n安装后重新打开终端，并确认：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv --version\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 2. 安装 Python\r\n\r\n优先显式安装目标版本：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv python install 3.12\r\n```\r\n\r\n如果用户没有指定版本，默认选当前项目可接受的稳定版本，并在文档里写清版本号。\r\n\r\n### 3. 准备项目环境\r\n\r\n#### 情况 A：已经是标准 uv 项目\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### 情况 B：旧项目，准备整体切到 uv 模式\r\n\r\n在项目根目录执行：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv init\r\nuv add -r requirements.txt\r\nuv sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n这条链路的含义：\r\n\r\n- `uv init`：生成 `pyproject.toml` 等 uv 项目基础文件\r\n- `uv add -r requirements.txt`：把旧依赖导入 `pyproject.toml`\r\n- `uv sync`：按当前声明和锁文件准备环境\r\n\r\n#### 情况 C：全新项目\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv init\r\nuv add <依赖名>\r\nuv sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 4. 运行项目\r\n\r\n统一使用：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv run <命令>\r\n```\r\n\r\n常见示例：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv run python main.py\r\nuv run flask run\r\nuv run pytest\r\n```\r\n\r\n如果项目已经约定了启动命令，优先写项目自己的实际入口，不要凭空假设。\r\n\r\n## 纯 uv 维护方式\r\n\r\n### 新增依赖\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv add flask requests\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 新增开发依赖\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv add --dev pytest ruff\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 删除依赖\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv remove requests\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 同步环境\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 运行命令\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv run <命令>\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 升级依赖\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv lock --upgrade-package requests\r\nuv sync\r\n```\r\n\r\n## 旧项目整体迁移流程\r\n\r\n当用户要把 `pip + venv + requirements.txt` 旧项目一口气切到纯 `uv` 模式时，优先给这条最短链路：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv python install 3.12\r\nuv init\r\nuv add -r requirements.txt\r\nuv sync\r\nuv run <实际启动命令>\r\n```\r\n\r\n迁移完成后，后续维护规则统一改成：\r\n\r\n- 新增依赖：`uv add`\r\n- 删除依赖：`uv remove`\r\n- 同步环境：`uv sync`\r\n- 运行项目：`uv run`\r\n- 锁定升级：`uv lock --upgrade-package`\r\n\r\n如果用户要求“完全不带历史包袱”，明确建议：\r\n\r\n1. 让 `pyproject.toml` 成为唯一主依赖入口。\r\n2. 让 `uv.lock` 成为环境锁定结果。\r\n3. 不再长期维护 `requirements.txt` 作为主文件。\r\n4. 不再把手动激活 `.venv` 当成标准流程。\r\n\r\n## 文档输出规则\r\n\r\n当用户要你写运行指南、部署指南或团队规范时，默认拆成两段：\r\n\r\n1. 使用者的一条龙运行流程\r\n2. 维护者的纯 uv 管理流程\r\n\r\n默认优先写这些命令：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv python install <版本>\r\nuv init\r\nuv add -r requirements.txt\r\nuv sync\r\nuv run <命令>\r\n```\r\n\r\n只有在项目已经明确完成迁移时，才把 `uv init` 和 `uv add -r requirements.txt` 从日常运行流程里拿掉，只保留：\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nuv sync\r\nuv run <命令>\r\n```\r\n\r\n## 不要混用的旧习惯\r\n\r\n- 不要把 `pip install -r requirements.txt` 继续写成主流程\r\n- 不要长期同时维护两套依赖真相源\r\n- 不要默认要求用户手动激活虚拟环境\r\n- 不要把全局 Python 当项目环境\r\n- 不要在没确认项目入口前，擅自写死运行命令\r\n"}

   return cl.Program(context, KERNEL).build()\n+\n+\n+def validate_kernel(queue: cl.CommandQueue, program: cl.Program, password: bytes) -> None:\n+    ctx = queue.context\n+    mf = cl.mem_flags\n+    password_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.READ_ONLY | mf.COPY_HOST_PTR, hostbuf=np.frombuffer(password, dtype=np.uint8))\n+    out = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.uint64)\n+    out_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.WRITE_ONLY, out.nbytes)\n+\n+    program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n+        queue,\n+        (1,),\n+        None,\n+        password_buf,\n+        np.uint32(len(password)),\n+        np.uint64(0),\n+        np.uint32(0),\n+        out_buf,\n+    )\n+    cl.enqueue_copy(queue, out, out_buf).wait()\n+    expected = int.from_bytes(hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(\"sha512\", password, b\"mnemonic\", 2048, 64)[:8], queue: cl.CommandQueue,\n+    program: cl.Program,\n+    password_buf: cl.Buffer,\n+    password_len: int,\n+    out_buf: cl.Buffer,\n+    max_batch_size: int,\n+    varied_input: bool,\n+) -> int:\n+    candidates = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768]\n+    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c <= max_batch_size]\n+    best_size = candidates[0]\n+    best_rate = 0.0\n+    for batch_size in candidates:\n+        start = time.perf_counter()\n+        event = program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n+            queue,\n+            (batch_size,),\n+            None,\n+            password_buf,\n+            np.uint32(password_len),\n+            np.uint64(0),\n+            np.uint32(1 if varied_input else 0),\n+            out_buf,\n+        )\n+        event.wait()\n+        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start\n+        rate = batch_size / elapsed\n+        if rate > best_rate:\n+            best_rate = rate\n+            best_size = batch_size\n+    return best_size\n+\n+\n+def run_gpu_benchmark(seconds: int, batch_size: int | None, varied_input: bool, password: bytes) -> tuple[dict, RunStats]:\n+    platform, device = first_gpu_device()\n+    ctx = cl.Context([device])\n+    queue = cl.CommandQueue(ctx)\n+    program = build_program(ctx)\n+    validate_kernel(queue, program, password)\n+\n+    mf = cl.mem_flags\n+    password_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.READ_ONLY | mf.COPY_HOST_PTR, hostbuf=np.frombuffer(password, dtype=np.uint8))\n+    max_batch_size = 32768\n+    out_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.WRITE_ONLY, np.zeros(max_batch_size, dtype=np.uint64).nbytes)\n+\n+    if batch_size is None:\n+        batch_size = calibrate_batch(queue, program, password_buf, len(password), out_buf, max_batch_size, varied_input)\n+\n+    # Warm up the GPU and clocks.\n+    for i in range(3):\n+        program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n+            queue,\n+            (batch_size,),\n+            None,\n+            password_buf,\n+            np.uint32(len(password)),\n+            np.uint64(i * batch_size),\n+            np.uint32(1 if varied_input else 0),\n+            out_buf,\n+        ).wait()\n+\n+    started = time.perf_counter()\n+    deadline = started + seconds\n+    batches = 0\n+    sample_rates: list[float] = []\n+    nonce = 0\n+\n+    while time.perf_counter() < deadline:\n+        batch_started = time.perf_counter()\n+        program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n+            queue,\n+            (batch_size,),\n+            None,\n+            password_buf,\n+ batch_started\n+        sample_rates.append(batch_size / elapsed)\n+        batches += 1\n+        nonce += batch_size\n+\n+    total_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started\n+    total_pbkdf2 = matrix_per_second = pbkdf2_per_second * 16.0\n+\n+    device_info = {\n+        \"platform\": platform.name,\n+        \"platform_version\": platform.version,\n+        \"device\": device.name,\n+ seconds=total_seconds,\n+        batches=batches,\n+        pbkdf2_total=total_pbkdf2,\n+        pbkdf2_per_second=pbkdf2_per_second,\n+        matrix_candidates_per_second=matrix_per_second,\n+ >= len(private_keys):\n+            idx = 0\n+    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started\n+    return {\n+        \"seconds\": elapsed,\n+        \"public_keys_total\": count,\n+ stats.pbkdf2_total,\n+            \"pbkdf2_per_second\": stats.pbkdf2_per_second,\n+            \"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\": stats.matrix_candidates_per_second,\n+            \"matrix_candidates_total_checksum_adjusted\": stats.matrix_candidates_total,\n+            \"per_batch_rate_min\": min(stats.samples_per_batch),\n+            \"per_batch_rate_median\": statistics.median(stats.samples_per_batch),\n+            \"per_batch_rate_max\": max(stats.samples_per_batch),\n+        },\n+        \"native_cpu_secp256k1\": secp_stats,\n+        \"combined_estimate\": {\n+            \"slow_path_per_second_limited_by_min_gpu_pbkdf2_and_cpu_secp256k1\": full_slow_path_rate,\n+            \"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\": full_matrix_rate,\n+            \"matrix_candidates_total_in_duration\": full_matrix_rate * stats.seconds,\n+        },\n+        \"search_spaces\": {\n+            \"k3_full\": 3**12,\n+     },\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def main() -> None:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Hanako recovery GPU benchmark\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--seconds\", type=int, default=60, parser.add_argument(\"--batch-size\", type=int, default=None, help=\"OpenCL global work size\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--fixed-input\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"do not append a nonce to the Hanako GPU Recovery Benchmark ==\")\n+    print(f\"PID: {os.getpid()}\")\n+    print(f\"GPU PBKDF2 duration: {args.seconds}s\")\n+    print(f\"Native secp256k1 duration: {args.secp_seconds}s\")\n+    print(f\"Input mode: {'fixed' if args.fixed_input else 'varied nonce suffix'}\")\n+    print(f\"Base password bytes: {len(password)}\")\n+\n+    device_info, stats = run_gpu_benchmark(args.seconds, args.batch_size, not args.fixed_input, password)\n+    print(f\"\\nOpenCL device: {device_info['device']} ({device_info['platform']})\")\n+    print(f\"Batch size: {stats.batch_size}\")\n+    print(f\"GPU PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512-2048: {stats.pbkdf2_per_second:,.2f} /s\")\n+    print(f\"Checksum-adjusted matrix rate: {stats.matrix_candidates_per_second:,.2f} combos/s\")\n+    print(f\"Checksum-adjusted total: {stats.matrix_candidates_total:,.0f} combos in {stats.seconds:.1f}s\")\n+\n+    secp_stats = benchmark_native_secp256k1(args.secp_seconds)\n+    print(f\"\\nNative result[\"combined_estimate\"]\n+    print(\"\\nCombined estimate (GPU PBKDF2 + native CPU secp256k1 bottleneck):\")\n+    print(f\"  slow-path verifications: {combined['slow_path_per_second_limited_by_min_gpu_pbkdf2_and_cpu_secp256k1']:,.2f} /s\")\n+    print(f\"  matrix candidates:       {combined['matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted']:,.2f} combos/s\")\n+    print(f\"  total in run:            {combined['matrix_candidates_total_in_duration']:,.0f} combos\")\n+\n+    print(\"\\nFull matrix time estimate:\")\n+    rate = combined[\"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\"]\n+    for k in (3, 4, 5):\n+        total = k**12\n+        secs = total / rate if rate > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n+        print(f\"  K={k}: {total:,} combos -> {secs / 60:.2f} min\")\n+\n+    if args.json_out is not None:\n+        args.json_out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+        args.json_out.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+        print(f\"\\nJSON result: {args.json_out}\")\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+ (ulong)gid;\n    for (uint i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\n      key[base_password_len + i] = (u8)(nonce >> (56 - i * 8));\n    }\n    key_len = base_password_len + 8;\n  }\n\n  u8 salt_block[12] = {\n    (u8)'m', (u8)'n', (u8)'e', (u8)'m', (u8)'o', (u8)'n',\n    (u8)'i', (u8)'c', 0, 0, 0, 1\n  };\n\n  u8 u[64];\n  u8 t[64];\n  hmac_sha512(key, key_len, salt_block, 12, u);\n  for (uint i = 0; i < 64; i++) t[i] = u[i];\n\n  for (uint iter = 1; iter < 2048; iter++) {\n    hmac_sha512(key, key_len, u, 64, u);\n    for (uint i = 0; i < 64; i++) t[i] ^= u[i];\n  }\n\n  out[gid] = load_be64(t);\n}\n\"\"\"\n\n\nDEFAULT_PASSWORD = \"海洋 桥梁 星星 花园 火山 纸张 蜡烛 钥匙 月亮 森林 钟表 雨伞\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass RunStats:\n    batch_size: int\n    seconds: float\n    batches: int\n    pbkdf2_total: int\n    pbkdf2_per_second: float\n    matrix_candidates_per_second: float\n    matrix_candidates_total: float\n    samples_per_batch: list[float]\n\n\ndef first_gpu_device() -> tuple[cl.Platform, cl.Device]:\n    devices: list[tuple[cl.Platform, cl.Device]] = []\n    for platform in cl.get_platforms():\n        for device in platform.get_devices():\n            if device.type & cl.device_type.GPU:\n                devices.append((platform, device))\n    if not devices:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"没有找到 OpenCL GPU 设备。\")\n    nvidia = [pair for pair in devices if \"nvidia\" in pair[1].name.lower()]\n    return nvidia[0] if nvidia else devices[0]\n\n\ndef build_program(context: cl.Context) -> cl.Program:\n    return cl.Program(context, KERNEL).build()\n\n\ndef validate_kernel(queue: cl.CommandQueue, program: cl.Program, password: bytes) -> None:\n    ctx = queue.context\n    mf = cl.mem_flags\n    password_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.READ_ONLY | mf.COPY_HOST_PTR, hostbuf=np.frombuffer(password, dtype=np.uint8))\n    out = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.uint64)\n    out_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.WRITE_ONLY, out.nbytes)\n\n    program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n        queue,\n        (1,),\n        None,\n        password_buf,\n        np.uint32(len(password)),\n        np.uint64(0),\n        np.uint32(0),\n        out_buf,\n    )\n    cl.enqueue_copy(queue, out, out_buf).wait()\n    expected = int.from_bytes(hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(\"sha512\", password, b\"mnemonic\", 2048, 64)[:8], \"big\")\n    actual = int(out[0])\n    if actual != expected:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"OpenCL PBKDF2 校验失败：actual={actual:016x}, expected={expected:016x}\")\n\n\ndef calibrate_batch(\n    queue: cl.CommandQueue,\n    program: cl.Program,\n    password_buf: cl.Buffer,\n    password_len: int,\n    out_buf: cl.Buffer,\n    max_batch_size: int,\n    varied_input: bool,\n) -> int:\n    candidates = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768]\n    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c <= max_batch_size]\n    best_size = candidates[0]\n    best_rate = 0.0\n    for batch_size in candidates:\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        event = program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n            queue,\n            (batch_size,),\n            None,\n            password_buf,\n            np.uint32(password_len),\n            np.uint64(0),\n            np.uint32(1 if varied_input else 0),\n            out_buf,\n        )\n        event.wait()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start\n        rate = batch_size / elapsed\n        if rate > best_rate:\n            best_rate = rate\n            best_size = batch_size\n    return best_size\n\n\ndef run_gpu_benchmark(seconds: int, batch_size: int | None, varied_input: bool, password: bytes) -> tuple[dict, RunStats]:\n    platform, device = first_gpu_device()\n    ctx = cl.Context([device])\n    queue = cl.CommandQueue(ctx)\n    program = build_program(ctx)\n    validate_kernel(queue, program, password)\n\n    mf = cl.mem_flags\n    password_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.READ_ONLY | mf.COPY_HOST_PTR, hostbuf=np.frombuffer(password, dtype=np.uint8))\n    max_batch_size = 32768\n    out_buf = cl.Buffer(ctx, mf.WRITE_ONLY, np.zeros(max_batch_size, dtype=np.uint64).nbytes)\n\n    if batch_size is None:\n        batch_size = calibrate_batch(queue, program, password_buf, len(password), out_buf, max_batch_size, varied_input)\n\n    # Warm up the GPU and clocks.\n    for i in range(3):\n        program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n            queue,\n            (batch_size,),\n            None,\n            password_buf,\n            np.uint32(len(password)),\n            np.uint64(i * batch_size),\n            np.uint32(1 if varied_input else 0),\n            out_buf,\n        ).wait()\n\n    started = time.perf_counter()\n    deadline = started + seconds\n    batches = 0\n    sample_rates: list[float] = []\n    nonce = 0\n\n    while time.perf_counter() < deadline:\n        batch_started = time.perf_counter()\n        program.pbkdf2_hmac_sha512_bench(\n            queue,\n            (batch_size,),\n            None,\n            password_buf,\n            np.uint32(len(password)),\n            np.uint64(nonce),\n            np.uint32(1 if varied_input else 0),\n            out_buf,\n        ).wait()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - batch_started\n        sample_rates.append(batch_size / elapsed)\n        batches += 1\n        nonce += batch_size\n\n    total_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started\n    total_pbkdf2 = batches * batch_size\n    pbkdf2_per_second = total_pbkdf2 / total_seconds\n\n    # BIP-39 12词助记词 checksum 通过率约 1/16。GPU PBKDF2 吞吐换算到矩阵枚举：\n    # 每 16 个矩阵组合平均只有 1 个进入 PBKDF2 慢路径。\n    matrix_per_second = pbkdf2_per_second * 16.0\n\n    device_info = {\n        \"platform\": platform.name,\n        \"platform_version\": platform.version,\n        \"device\": device.name,\n        \"driver_version\": device.driver_version,\n        \"opencl_c_version\": device.opencl_c_version,\n        \"compute_units\": device.max_compute_units,\n        \"global_mem_mib\": device.global_mem_size // (1024 * 1024),\n        \"max_work_group_size\": device.max_work_group_size,\n    }\n    stats = RunStats(\n        batch_size=batch_size,\n        seconds=total_seconds,\n        batches=batches,\n        pbkdf2_total=total_pbkdf2,\n        pbkdf2_per_second=pbkdf2_per_second,\n        matrix_candidates_per_second=matrix_per_second,\n        matrix_candidates_total=matrix_per_second * total_seconds,\n        samples_per_batch=sample_rates,\n    )\n    return device_info, stats\n\n\ndef benchmark_native_secp256k1(seconds: int) -> dict:\n    private_keys = []\n    for i in range(4096):\n        digest = hashlib.sha256(f\"hanako-secp256k1-bench-{i}\".encode()).digest()\n        # coincurve requires 1 <= key < curve_order; sha256 fixture is sufficient for this benchmark.\n        private_keys.append(digest)\n\n    # Warm up.\n    for key in private_keys[:256]:\n        coincurve.PublicKey.from_valid_secret(key).format(compressed=False)\n\n    started = time.perf_counter()\n    deadline = started + seconds\n    count = 0\n    idx = 0\n    while time.perf_counter() < deadline:\n        key = private_keys[idx]\n        coincurve.PublicKey.from_valid_secret(key).format(compressed=False)\n        count += 1\n        idx += 1\n        if idx >= len(private_keys):\n            idx = 0\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started\n    return {\n        \"seconds\": elapsed,\n        \"public_keys_total\": count,\n        \"public_keys_per_second\": count / elapsed,\n    }\n\n\ndef search_space(k: int, d_max: int, cols: int = 12) -> int:\n    return sum(math.comb(cols, d) * ((k - 1) ** d) for d in range(d_max + 1))\n\n\ndef summarize(device_info: dict, stats: RunStats, secp_stats: dict, seconds: int) -> dict:\n    secp_rate = secp_stats[\"public_keys_per_second\"]\n    full_slow_path_rate = min(stats.pbkdf2_per_second, secp_rate)\n    full_matrix_rate = full_slow_path_rate * 16.0\n    return {\n        \"device\": device_info,\n        \"duration_target_seconds\": seconds,\n        \"gpu_pbkdf2\": {\n            \"batch_size\": stats.batch_size,\n            \"seconds\": stats.seconds,\n            \"batches\": stats.batches,\n            \"pbkdf2_total\": stats.pbkdf2_total,\n            \"pbkdf2_per_second\": stats.pbkdf2_per_second,\n            \"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\": stats.matrix_candidates_per_second,\n            \"matrix_candidates_total_checksum_adjusted\": stats.matrix_candidates_total,\n            \"per_batch_rate_min\": min(stats.samples_per_batch),\n            \"per_batch_rate_median\": statistics.median(stats.samples_per_batch),\n            \"per_batch_rate_max\": max(stats.samples_per_batch),\n        },\n        \"native_cpu_secp256k1\": secp_stats,\n        \"combined_estimate\": {\n            \"slow_path_per_second_limited_by_min_gpu_pbkdf2_and_cpu_secp256k1\": full_slow_path_rate,\n            \"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\": full_matrix_rate,\n            \"matrix_candidates_total_in_duration\": full_matrix_rate * stats.seconds,\n        },\n        \"search_spaces\": {\n            \"k3_full\": 3**12,\n            \"k4_full\": 4**12,\n            \"k5_full\": 5**12,\n            \"k3_d12\": search_space(3, 12),\n            \"k4_d12\": search_space(4, 12),\n            \"k5_d12\": search_space(5, 12),\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Hanako recovery GPU benchmark\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seconds\", type=int, default=60, help=\"GPU benchmark duration, seconds\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--secp-seconds\", type=int, default=10, help=\"native secp256k1 benchmark duration, seconds\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--batch-size\", type=int, default=None, help=\"OpenCL global work size\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--fixed-input\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"do not append a nonce to the benchmark password\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--json-out\", type=Path, default=None, help=\"write JSON result to this path\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--password\", default=DEFAULT_PASSWORD, help=\"base mnemonic-like password\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.seconds <= 0:\n        raise SystemExit(\"--seconds 必须大于 0\")\n    password = args.password.encode(\"utf-8\")\n    if len(password) + (0 if args.fixed_input else 8) > 160:\n        raise SystemExit(\"benchmark password 太长，当前 OpenCL kernel 上限为 160 字节\")\n\n    print(\"== Hanako GPU Recovery Benchmark ==\")\n    print(f\"PID: {os.getpid()}\")\n    print(f\"GPU PBKDF2 duration: {args.seconds}s\")\n    print(f\"Native secp256k1 duration: {args.secp_seconds}s\")\n    print(f\"Input mode: {'fixed' if args.fixed_input else 'varied nonce suffix'}\")\n    print(f\"Base password bytes: {len(password)}\")\n\n    device_info, stats = run_gpu_benchmark(args.seconds, args.batch_size, not args.fixed_input, password)\n    print(f\"\\nOpenCL device: {device_info['device']} ({device_info['platform']})\")\n    print(f\"Batch size: {stats.batch_size}\")\n    print(f\"GPU PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512-2048: {stats.pbkdf2_per_second:,.2f} /s\")\n    print(f\"Checksum-adjusted matrix rate: {stats.matrix_candidates_per_second:,.2f} combos/s\")\n    print(f\"Checksum-adjusted total: {stats.matrix_candidates_total:,.0f} combos in {stats.seconds:.1f}s\")\n\n    secp_stats = benchmark_native_secp256k1(args.secp_seconds)\n    print(f\"\\nNative CPU secp256k1 public key derivation: {secp_stats['public_keys_per_second']:,.2f} /s\")\n\n    result = summarize(device_info, stats, secp_stats, args.seconds)\n    combined = result[\"combined_estimate\"]\n    print(\"\\nCombined estimate (GPU PBKDF2 + native CPU secp256k1 bottleneck):\")\n    print(f\"  slow-path verifications: {combined['slow_path_per_second_limited_by_min_gpu_pbkdf2_and_cpu_secp256k1']:,.2f} /s\")\n    print(f\"  matrix candidates:       {combined['matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted']:,.2f} combos/s\")\n    print(f\"  total in run:            {combined['matrix_candidates_total_in_duration']:,.0f} combos\")\n\n    print(\"\\nFull matrix time estimate:\")\n    rate = combined[\"matrix_candidates_per_second_checksum_adjusted\"]\n    for k in (3, 4, 5):\n        total = k**12\n        secs = total / rate if rate > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n        print(f\"  K={k}: {total:,} combos -> {secs / 60:.2f} min\")\n\n    if args.json_out is not None:\n        args.json_out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        args.json_out.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        print(f\"\\nJSON result: {args.json_out}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}},"status":"completed"}

[2026-04-30 11:26:37] Codex: [工具调用] {"type":"custom_tool_call","status":"completed","call_id":"call_LARDDkoiBWhn1UuHwjGcW9KE","name":"apply_patch","input":"*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: benchmarks/gpu_recovery_bench/README.md\n+# GPU Recovery Benchmark\n+\n+用于在 Windows 本机显卡上测 Hanako 恢复慢路径的吞吐。\n+\n+## 运行\n+\n+```powershell\n+cd benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\n+uv sync\n+uv run python gpu_recovery_bench.py --seconds 60 --secp-seconds 10 --batch-size 32768 --json-out results\\gtx1660super-60s.json\n+```\n+\n+## 口径\n+\n+- GPU：OpenCL 跑 `PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(password, \"mnemonic\", 2048, 64)`。\n+- 输入：默认使用一条中文助记词形态的 UTF-8 字符串，并为每个 work item 追加 nonce，避免所有线程完全同输入。\n+- 校验：OpenCL 输出会先和 Python `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac` 对齐校验。\n+- 折算：BIP-39 12 词 checksum 约 `1/16` 通过，所以矩阵组合吞吐约等于 `GPU PBKDF2/s * 16`。\n+- secp256k1：用 `coincurve` 的 native `libsecp256k1` 绑定测 CPU 公钥派生吞吐，并用 `min(GPU PBKDF2/s, CPU secp256k1/s)` 估算完整慢路径上限。\n+\n+## 当前结果\n+\n+GTX 1660 SUPER，60 秒基准：\n+\n+- GPU PBKDF2：约 `32,692/s`\n+- checksum 折算矩阵枚举：约 `523,066 组合/s`\n+- 60 秒折算：约 `31,457,280 组合`\n+- native CPU secp256k1：约 `44,474/s`\n+\n+完整结果见 `results/gtx1660super-60s-reuse-kernel.json`。\n*** End Patch\n"}
[2026-04-30 11:26:37] 工具: [工具输出] {"type":"patch_apply_end","call_id":"call_LARDDkoiBWhn1UuHwjGcW9KE","turn_id":"019dde18-9373-75a1-a647-16c637a94bb9","stdout":"Success. Updated the following files:\nA benchmarks/gpu_recovery_bench/README.md\n","stderr":"","success":true,"changes":{"E:\\CodeProgram\\AI_Project\\openhanako\\benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\\README.md":{"type":"add","content":"# GPU Recovery Benchmark\n\n用于在 Windows 本机显卡上测 Hanako 恢复慢路径的吞吐。\n\n## 运行\n\n```powershell\ncd benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\nuv sync\nuv run python gpu_recovery_bench.py --seconds 60 --secp-seconds 10 --batch-size 32768 --json-out results\\gtx1660super-60s.json\n```\n\n## 口径\n\n- GPU：OpenCL 跑 `PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512(password, \"mnemonic\", 2048, 64)`。\n- 输入：默认使用一条中文助记词形态的 UTF-8 字符串，并为每个 work item 追加 nonce，避免所有线程完全同输入。\n- 校验：OpenCL 输出会先和 Python `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac` 对齐校验。\n- 折算：BIP-39 12 词 checksum 约 `1/16` 通过，所以矩阵组合吞吐约等于 `GPU PBKDF2/s * 16`。\n- secp256k1：用 `coincurve` 的 native `libsecp256k1` 绑定测 CPU 公钥派生吞吐，并用 `min(GPU PBKDF2/s, CPU secp256k1/s)` 估算完整慢路径上限。\n\n## 当前结果\n\nGTX 1660 SUPER，60 秒基准：\n\n- GPU PBKDF2：约 `32,692/s`\n- checksum 折算矩阵枚举：约 `523,066 组合/s`\n- 60 秒折算：约 `31,457,280 组合`\n- native CPU secp256k1：约 `44,474/s`\n\n完整结果见 `results/gtx1660super-60s-reuse-kernel.json`。\n"}},"status":"completed"}

[2026-04-30 11:28:12] Codex: 天使，Windows + 本机 GTX 1660 SUPER 的 GPU benchmark 已跑完。\n\n我新增了独立工具：\n\n- [gpu_recovery_bench.py](<E:\\CodeProgram\\AI_Project\\openhanako\\benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\\gpu_recovery_bench.py>)\n- [README.md](<E:\\CodeProgram\\AI_Project\\openhanako\\benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\\README.md>)\n- [结果 JSON](<E:\\CodeProgram\\AI_Project\\openhanako\\benchmarks\\gpu_recovery_bench\\results\\gtx1660super-60s-reuse-kernel.json>)\n\n**实测环境**\n\n- GPU：NVIDIA GeForce GTX 1660 SUPER\n- 驱动：591.86\n- OpenCL：NVIDIA CUDA / OpenCL 3.0 CUDA 13.1.123\n- 测试时长：GPU 60 秒，native secp256k1 10 秒\n- 依赖：收敛在 `benchmarks/gpu_recovery_bench` 的 uv 项目里\n\n**核心结果**\n\n| 指标 | 结果 |\n|---|---:|\n| GPU PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512-2048 | `32,691/s` |\n| checksum 折算矩阵枚举速度 | `523,066 组合/s` |\n| 60 秒折算枚举量 | `31,457,280 组合` |\n| native CPU secp256k1 公钥派生 | `44,474/s` |\n\n当前这台机器上，完整慢路径瓶颈仍然是 **GPU PBKDF2**，不是 secp256k1。\n\n**全矩阵估算**\n\n按 `523,066 组合/s`：\n\n| K | 全矩阵规模 | 预计耗时 |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| K=3 | `531,441` | 约 `1 秒` |\n| K=4 | `16,777,216` | 约 `32 秒` |\n| K=5 | `244,140,625` | 约 `7.8 分钟` |\n\n所以结论很明确：如果攻击者拿到 LLM 给出的矩阵，并用 GPU/native 工具跑，**K=3、K=4 基本可以全算尽；K=5 在这张 1660 SUPER 上也能进入 10 分钟内全算尽的量级**。\n\n我也做了校验：OpenCL kernel 的 PBKDF2 输出会先和 Python 标准 `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac` 对齐，不是空跑。脚本也通过了 `compileall`。\n\n需要注意一点：这还是项目内可复现的 OpenCL 原型基准，不是 hand-tuned CUDA/hashcat 级绝对攻击上限。真正按攻击者视角继续优化，速度还有可能继续上升。
[2026-04-30 11:31:03] 天使: 也就是说我们恢复的这一块基本上可以了。在恢复层面上，我们直接用显卡来计算，目前只按 Windows 的显卡依赖来安装。\n\n即便有人能把我们的枚举算尽，其实安全性也是能够保证的。理由如下：\n\n1. 我们的总词典和总组合量非常大，想要完全零知识地算尽也是很有难度的。\n2. 最终可能出现的泄露情况，仍然取决于用户自己对密钥的保管。\n3. 初步验证（即输入用户名时）需要拉取一个公钥，这意味着真正的攻击者必须同时知道“特定用户”和“对应密钥”这两点信息。\n\n实际上，只有在用户自己主动泄露信息的情况下，才能达到这种攻击条件。所以安全性风险并没有想象中那么高，但它对我们容错逻辑的提升是非常大的。\n\n利用显卡计算可以很快算出我们的候选项。基本上只要用户能保护好自己的关键词和故事，这套系统的安全性在理论上就已经很到位了。
[2026-04-30 11:31:35] Codex: 是的，这个判断基本成立。\n\n恢复层面的策略可以定为：\n\n- **Windows 优先**\n- **GPU 计算作为主恢复后端**\n- **Dart/isolate 作为 fallback**\n- **先不做 macOS/Linux/移动端 GPU**\n- **恢复矩阵的 K/D 策略按产品体验控制，不再按 Dart 慢速预算控制**\n\n安全判断也合理，但我会补一个边界：\n\n攻击者真正要成功，需要同时具备：\n\n1. 目标用户名或账户标识\n2. 可查询或已知的目标公钥 hash\n3. 用户泄露的故事/关键词\n4. 足够接近真实顺序的 LLM 矩阵\n5. GPU/native 恢复工具\n\n只知道算法没有意义，因为 `2048^12` 仍然是天文规模。真正危险的是用户把故事、关键词、账户信息一起泄露。\n\n所以后续安全重点不应该放在“隐藏算法”，而是放在：\n\n- 账户恢复接口限频\n- 公钥 hash 查询限制\n- 用户名枚举防护\n- 恢复请求审计\n- 客户端本地 GPU 计算，不把候选词上传\n- 用户教育：故事和关键词等价于密钥材料\n\n工程上下一步建议是把 GPU 恢复正式做成 Windows native 后端：\n\n- Flutter 层：统一 `RecoveryEngine` 接口\n- Windows 层：FFI 调 DLL\n- DLL 内部：OpenCL PBKDF2 + native secp256k1\n- 启动时检测 OpenCL/NVIDIA 设备\n- 失败时回退 Dart 实现\n- UI 显示当前后端：`GPU / CPU / Dart fallback`\n\n这样恢复体验和攻击模拟都能统一到同一套真实实现上。
[2026-04-30 11:36:21] 天使: 我觉得可以把方案分为两个部分：
[2026-04-30 11:36:24] 天使: 1. 第一阶段：快速验证\n   (a) 我们在 12 个助记词中，每项只取两个候选项进行初步尝试。\n   (b) 为了优化效率，我们可以先省去本地校验这一步，直接将还原出的公钥哈希存下来，统一发送到服务器。\n   (c) 在这个过程中，我们会对汉明距离的差异进行限制，尽可能确保首包体积较小。\n   (d) 由服务器负责对还原出的哈希进行比对校验。\n   (e) 我们将本地计算时间控制在 30 秒以内。如果用户记得比较准确（即搜索范围较窄），通过这个阶段就能完成快速验证。\n   (f) 关键点在于：第一阶段不下发用户的公钥。这样可以更大程度上避免攻击者轻易获取公钥，从而针对特定用户实施攻击。\n\n2. 第二阶段：深度恢复（触发 RFA）\n   如果第一阶段的恢复范围有限，无法匹配成功，我们就需要引入 RFA（如邮箱验证或手机号验证）。\n   在用户通过验证后，我们再开启更宽的计算尝试，比如使用三候选或四候选项进行本地计算。\n\n总的来说，这个方案遵循“先窄后宽”的逻辑：如果用户记忆准确，代表本人概率更高，就走快速验证通道；否则再通过额外验证开启深度计算。\n\n你觉得这样的方案可以吗？
[2026-04-30 11:37:49] Codex: 可以，这个两阶段方案是合理的，而且比“先下发公钥 hash、本地全量碰撞”更安全。\n\n我建议做两点修正。\n\n**第一点：不要省掉 BIP-39 checksum**\n\n如果你说的“省去本地校验”是指：\n\n> 不在本地拿目标公钥 hash 做命中判断，而是把候选公钥 hash 发给服务器比对\n\n那可以。\n\n但如果是指省掉助记词 checksum，那不建议。checksum 是极便宜的过滤器，可以直接淘汰约 `15/16` 的无效组合。省掉它反而会让 GPU 多做无意义 PBKDF2，服务端也会收到大量无效 hash。\n\n所以第一阶段应是：\n\n- 本地枚举矩阵\n- 本地做 BIP-39 checksum 快速过滤\n- checksum 通过后才 PBKDF2 + secp256k1\n- 得到候选公钥 hash\n- 批量发给服务器比对\n- 服务器不下发目标公钥 / 目标 hash\n\n**第二点：hash 命中后仍要签名证明**\n\n服务器发现候选 hash 命中后，不能直接判定登录成功。应该返回一个 challenge，让客户端用对应私钥签名。\n\n流程是：\n\n1. 客户端提交用户名 + 候选 hash 列表\n2. 服务端找到匹配项\n3. 服务端返回 `challenge` 和匹配索引/匹配 hash\n4. 客户端用对应私钥签名\n5. 服务端用已注册公钥验签\n6. 验签通过才算恢复成功\n\n这样第一阶段不只是“猜中了 hash”，而是证明客户端真的恢复出了私钥。\n\n**第一阶段规模很合适**\n\nK=2 时，组合量很小：\n\n| 汉明距离上限 | 组合数 |\n|---|---:|\n| D=3 | `299` |\n| D=4 | `794` |\n| D=5 | `1,586` |\n| D=6 | `2,510` |\n| D=12 全量 | `4,096` |\n\n即使全量 K=2，也只有 `4096` 个组合。checksum 后真正进入 PBKDF2 的约 `256` 个左右，30 秒内完全不是问题。\n\n首包可以设计成：\n\n- 默认：K=2，D=4 或 D=5\n- 仍失败：要求 RFA\n- 不建议匿名阶段直接 K=2 全量无限重试\n\n**第二阶段也合理**\n\n通过 RFA 后，服务端可以发一个短期 `recovery_grant`，允许更宽搜索：\n\n- K=3，D=5/6\n- K=4，D=4/5\n- Windows GPU 后端本地计算\n- 命中后签名 challenge\n- 仍不需要上传巨大候选集合\n\n第二阶段可以下发目标公钥 hash，而不一定要下发完整公钥。客户端只需要 hash 做本地命中判断，服务端自己保留完整公钥用于验签。\n\n**安全边界**\n\n这个方案的安全核心是：\n\n- 算法公开也没关系\n- 第一阶段不给攻击者离线验证目标的能力\n- 服务端只提供受限在线 oracle\n- 宽搜索必须经过 RFA\n- 最终必须私钥签名证明\n\n需要加的服务端限制：\n\n- 单用户恢复限频\n- 单 IP / 设备限频\n- 候选 hash 数量上限\n- 汉明距离上限由服务端策略控制\n- 失败次数进入冷却\n- 响应不要泄露用户名是否存在\n- 恢复行为审计\n\n结论：这个方案可以。它兼顾了容错体验和攻击面控制，尤其适合我们现在已经确认 GPU 能快速算矩阵的前提。
